# Phase 1 progress

Budget: 2026-10-21 to 2027-02-24 (pre-registration §9). Plan: [phase1-plan.md](phase1-plan.md).

## Modules M1–M5

Each module: 3 attempts in Track F (pre-registration §5.2), logged here with gates and diffs. Porting infrastructure: `harness/port/modules.mjs` (unit list per module from the slice), `harness/port/gate.mjs` (Gate 2 + module gate, run by attempts and orchestrator), `tests/NeverSharp.Faithful.Tests` (module gates that are not dump comparisons), vectors from tsc 6.0.3 (`harness/port/vectors-*.mjs`).

### M1: Core

Brief: [modules/M1.md](modules/M1.md); units: [modules/M1-units.md](modules/M1-units.md) (91 units, 889 lines). Gate: Gate 2 + 8 unit tests over about 690,000 calls compared with tsc 6.0.3 (every file name of the equivalence corpus, path edge cases, spelling suggestions). Harness checked with a deliberately wrong stub before the attempts (builds under Gate 2, fails the tests with readable diffs).

| Attempt | Model | Gate runs | Gate (orchestrator rerun) | Median (99.9 % CI) | Allocated | Rank | Commit |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| 1 | Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) | 2 | pass | **4.884 ms** (4.871–4.922) | **5.27 MB** | **1, merged** | b1ea711 → main |
| 2 | Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) | 1 | pass | 5.323 ms (5.310–5.339) | 6.06 MB | 2 | 74394cb |
| 3 | Sonnet 5.5 (`claude-sonnet-5-5`) | 4 (3 failed on analyzer/header findings) | pass | 5.380 ms (5.360–5.393) | 6.06 MB | 3 | 7295657 |

Ranking workload and job as fixed in the brief (`bench/NeverSharp.Benchmarks`, `M1.ParseSourceFilePathWork`: the path work of `parseSourceFile` for the 24,511 file names of the tuning corpus; BenchmarkDotNet 0.15.8, NativeAOT, 3 warmup + 20 iterations, outliers removed by BenchmarkDotNet; 2026-10-21, attempts measured one after another). Raw reports: `results/ranking/M1/`. The intervals do not overlap, so the first criterion decides. Attempt branches kept as `attempts/M1/attempt-1..3`. Two fixes in the benchmark project before the measured run, neither touching attempt code: the Visual Studio Installer directory on PATH for the NativeAOT linker, and `NoWarn CS0649` (the compiler skips this check for internal fields in the Faithful project because of `InternalsVisibleTo`; the benchmark compiles the sources into an assembly without it).

Units left out by all three (allowed by the brief, no meaning for C# values): `emptyMap`, `hasOwnProperty`, `hasProperty`, `isArray`, `isString`; attempt 3 also `toArray`. Common deviations: exceptions are `InvalidOperationException` with TypeScript's exact message; `Debug.getFunctionName` gives `""` (reflection is banned); regular expressions replaced by hand-written matching; `assertionCache` records levels instead of swapping functions for `noop`.

**Open item for later modules:** all three map `toUpperCase`/`toLowerCase` to .NET's simple invariant case mapping; JavaScript uses full Unicode case mapping (`ß` → `SS`). Used by `getSpellingSuggestion` and `equateStringsCaseInsensitive`; Gate 1 shows whether it is reached.

### M2: Scanner

Brief: [modules/M2.md](modules/M2.md); units: [modules/M2-units.md](modules/M2-units.md) (67 units, 3,854 lines). Gate: Gate 2, unit tests (M1 + M2: about 1.4 million calls of the exported scanner helpers compared with tsc 6.0.3, `harness/port/vectors-m2.mjs`), G1a on all 50,177 units. Test plumbing checked with a deliberately wrong stub before the attempts.

| Attempt | Model | Gate runs | Gate (orchestrator rerun) | Median (99.9 % CI) | Allocated | Rank | Commit |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| 1 | Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) | 2 | pass, G1a 50,177/50,177 | **186.6 ms** (185.7–187.0) | **89.3 MB** | **1, merged** | 0d2fb3b → main |
| 2 | Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) | 4 (3 + 1 correction round) | pass, G1a 50,177/50,177 | 225.9 ms (224.9–226.7) | 121.0 MB | 2 | a3b46c2, ea0afea |
| 3 | Sonnet 5.5 (`claude-sonnet-5-5`) | 1 counted (see below) | pass, G1a 50,177/50,177 | 228.1 ms (227.6–228.8) | 93.5 MB | 3 | 13661b5 |

Ranking as fixed in the brief (`M2.ScanTuningCorpus`, BenchmarkDotNet 0.15.8, NativeAOT, 3 warmup + 20 iterations; 2026-10-21, one after another on an otherwise idle machine). Raw reports: `results/ranking/M2/`; branches `attempts/M2/attempt-1..3`. Intervals do not overlap; the first criterion decides.

Events and decisions:

- **Rule 3, compiler hints (decided by the author, 2026-10-21):** attempt 2 had put `[MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]` on 16 leaf helpers, citing the Go compiler's automatic inlining, and flagged the claim itself. Decision: strict reading — a compiler or JIT hint has no counterpart in typescript-go's code (Go has no force-inline directive), so it is not allowed in Track F; such tuning belongs to Track O. Attempt 2 removed the attributes itself in a correction round within its gate budget (23 deleted lines, nothing added; gate passed), then all three were measured. Future briefs state the rule explicitly.
- **Other optimizations** (all three replaced quadratic string `+=` by builders, citing `strings.Builder`/parts + `strings.Join` in `internal/scanner/scanner.go`; attempt 1 also ASCII fast paths in `scanIdentifier` and after line breaks, attempt 2 `numberCache` and `scanHexDigits`): counterparts checked in typescript-go's `scanner.go`, they exist.
- **Benchmark harness fix before the measured run:** BenchmarkDotNet's dependency `Microsoft.Diagnostics.Tracing.TraceEvent` exports a top-level namespace `Diagnostics` that hid the generated `Diagnostics` class (reported by attempt 3, which had added a using alias in its own code); fixed with an extern alias in the benchmark project (8e407ff), identical for all attempts.
- **Gate-run counting:** attempt 3 ran the gate's parts (tests, dump, compare) by hand before its single counted gate run; the brief did not define hand runs of the parts, so its count is not comparable. From M3 on, any run of a gate part counts.
- **Independence caveat:** the three attempts shared one scratch directory (the session's); top-level helper files collided (`gen-tables.mjs`, `tables.cs.txt`, `gen-ch.mjs`: generators of the Unicode identifier tables and character-code constants, i.e. data generated from `scanner.ts`, not scanner logic). Attempt 1 noticed an overwrite and moved to a subfolder. Whether an attempt read another's helper output cannot be ruled out. From M3 on, each attempt gets its own scratch folder.
- **Testing beyond the gate:** all three attempts wrote their own differential tests against tsc 6.0.3 for the paths G1a does not reach (rescans, regex validator, JSX, JSDoc scanning, lookahead), each reporting 0 differences (attempt 3 found and fixed a `toString(8)` bug for huge legacy octal literals this way). These are the attempts' own checks, not gates; G1 (M4/M5) covers these paths.

### M3 + M4: AST factory and parser

Brief: [modules/M34.md](modules/M34.md); units: [modules/M34-units.md](modules/M34-units.md) (951 units, 13,303 lines). Gate: Gate 2, unit tests, G1a, G1 with `JSDocParsingMode.ParseNone` against tsc's ParseNone dump on all 50,177 units (plan amendment in phase1-plan.md §4: JSDoc parsing changes non-JSDoc output in 279 units, so stripping the JSDoc section from the ParseAll dump would not be equivalent). Limit 100 gate runs.

Events:

- **All three attempts stopped on the account's monthly spend limit** (HTTP 429) about 50 minutes in, before any build or gate run; resumed after the author's go-ahead. Wall-clock durations of these attempts are therefore not comparable with M1/M2.
- **Brief corrected during the attempts (orchestrator error):** the brief excluded `createSyntaxCursor` as incremental parsing, but `reparseTopLevelAwait` reuses nodes through it (`parser.ts:1864`), so files with top-level `await` need it. Attempt 1 found this and ported it anyway. The unit list and brief were corrected (951 units) and the same correction was sent to all three attempts when they were resumed.

| Attempt | Model | Gate runs | Gate (orchestrator rerun) | Median (99.9 % CI) | Allocated | Rank | Commit |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| 1 | Opus 5.5 (+ sub-agents) | 0 | stopped before finishing (author, token cost); never built | — | — | — | attempts/M34/attempt-1 (incomplete) |
| 2 | Opus 5.5 (+ 5 sub-agents) | 5 | pass, G1 ParseNone 50,177/50,177 | **2,476 ms** (2,460–2,496) | **726 MB** | **1, merged** | b7a8826 → main |
| 3 | Sonnet 5.5 (+ 11 sub-agents) | 6 + 1 correction round | pass, G1 ParseNone 50,177/50,177 | 2,532 ms (2,523–2,548) | 1,009 MB | 2 | 325a8e5, c03c38f |

Ranking as fixed in the brief (`M34.ParseTuningCorpus`, NativeAOT, 3 warmup + 20 iterations, one after another); raw reports `results/ranking/M34/`. Intervals do not overlap.

More events:

- **Static state (decided by the author: strict reading):** attempt 3 kept TypeScript's module-level `intersectingChangeSet` as a thread-safe static `ConditionalWeakTable`; the brief allows no mutable static state besides the parser pool. The original agent could not be resumed (stopped by an interruption), so a fresh agent (Sonnet 5.5, medium effort, no sub-agents) made only this change in attempt 3's worktree (per-parser `HashSet`, cleared in `ClearState`; 12 lines added, 14 removed; gate passed) before the ranking.
- **Virtual dispatch:** attempt 3's `Node.NameForTransformFlags` (virtual, 58 overrides) has its counterpart in typescript-go's per-type `nodeData.Name()` (`internal/ast/ast.go:209`, `:1215`); accepted under rule 3.
- **Attempt 1 stopped** by the author for token cost after the spend-limit pause; its unfinished, never-built work is kept on `attempts/M34/attempt-1`. From M5 on, two attempts per module (prereg/v3).
- Both finished attempts reached 50,177/50,177 on their first full gate run (attempt 2 after two hand builds; attempt 3 after three builds), i.e. the "long tail of error recovery" the plan feared did not materialise for the ParseNone gate.

|---|---|---:|---|---|---:|---:|---|

| 2 | Sonnet 5.5 (`claude-sonnet-5-5`), medium | 2 | pass, Gate 1 50,177/50,177 | 2,486 ms (2,471–2,507) | 738.1 MB | 2 | c0d715d |

**Gate 1 complete for Track F** (2026-10-21): the merged parser reproduces tsc 6.0.3's canonical AST dump, JSDoc section included, on all 50,177 units of the equivalence corpus; all earlier gates still pass on main.

Ranking (`M5.ParseTuningCorpus`, ParseForTypeErrors, NativeAOT): the 99.9 % intervals overlap slightly (2,471–2,474 ms); pre-registration §5.2 ranks Track F by the median first, so attempt 1 is first; allocation is practically equal. Events:

- **Generator defect found by the attempts:** `JSDocSignature.parameters` is declared `readonly JSDocParameterTag[]` in types.ts, but the parser also stores other JSDoc tags there (`@this` inside `@callback`, unit 012584 callbackTag4.ts). Attempt 2 (finished first) patched the generated file to pass, against the brief's letter; the orchestrator fixed the generator (field `Node[]?`, 48464cc) and offered exactly this change to attempt 1 while it was running, which used it. Both attempts thus made the same, now sanctioned change; the merge takes the generated file from the generator.
- Attempt 1's one optimization (setting `NodeFlags.JSDoc` in the context directly instead of `doInsideOfContext` with a closure) has its counterpart in typescript-go's `parseJSDocComment` (`internal/parser/jsdoc.go`, saves and sets `p.contextFlags`).

## M6: measurement (Track F)

**Verdict (2026-10-22, Windows only): no parity.** G = 6.31 [6.20, 6.41] for `cs-aot-ws` against `tsgo7-parse` (lower bound > 1.20): Track F parses about 6.3 × slower than typescript-go, about 1.9 × faster than tsc 6.0.3. Deciding run: [results/m6/2026-10-22T07-01-02-524Z/VERDICT.md](../results/m6/2026-10-22T07-01-02-524Z/VERDICT.md) (run 3, after prereg/v4).

- Runs 1 and 2 (rig-compliant) failed the then-binding variance gate only in JIT/Server-GC "single-threaded" configurations; amendment prereg/v4 (decided after seeing both, disclosed) binds the gate to tsc6-parse, tsgo7-parse-single and cs-aot-ws-single. All three runs are archived in `results/m6/`; the primary ratio was 6.45, 6.28 and 6.31.
- Main cost driver visible in every run: garbage collection (e.g. vscode, cs-aot-ws: about 7.5 s summed GC pauses in an 8.5 s run; Server GC halves the wall-clock). This is where Track O (arenas, NoAlloc) starts.
- Development measurements from here on are short (module benchmarks, probes, `dotnet-trace` 10.0.750501 in `.tools/dotnet-trace`); the next protocol run is the Track O endpoint.

## Track O

Profile of Track F and the resulting step order: [track-o-profile.md](track-o-profile.md). Steps evaluated and not evaluated (A9): [track-o-steps.md](track-o-steps.md).

**Infrastructure (2026-10-22):**

- **Seed (step O0):** `tools/NeverSharp.Generate/seed-optimized.mjs` copied the hand-written Track F modules (49 files) into `src/NeverSharp.Optimized/` with the namespace changed; the generator emits the node classes for both tracks until step O6. Track O builds clean under its own Gate 2 (Track O banned list, NoAlloc analyzer NS001–NS015 as errors).
- **One set of tools for both tracks:** `tools/NeverSharp.Dump.Optimized` and `tests/NeverSharp.Optimized.Tests` compile the same dump writers and unit tests against Track O; the parse driver (`build-cs.mjs --tracks optimized`, the `cs-o-*` configurations) and the benchmarks (`-p:NeverSharpTrack=Optimized`) take Track O as well.
- **Gate:** `gate.mjs --track optimized` runs everything (Gate 2, unit tests, G1a, G1 ParseNone, Gate 1). The seed passes: 50,177 of 50,177 units in every dump comparison. Track F still passes its full gate.
- **NoAlloc coverage:** `harness/port/noalloc-report.mjs` counts annotated methods, lifted constraints and `[NoAllocUnchecked]` exceptions; the seed: 0 of 1,555 methods annotated.

### O6a: handle-based node API

Brief: [modules/O6a.md](modules/O6a.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, no sub-agents; mechanical conversion with a one-off Roslyn rewriter in its scratch folder), about 31 of 100 gate runs. Commit 6ba80cf (branch `attempts/O6a`), merged.

- **Design:** nodes are `readonly struct Node` (one `int`); `NodeStore` per parse holds one 40-byte record per node (kind, flags, pos, end, parent, slot offsets, transform flags, modifier-flags cache, original) plus per-kind blocks of int slots (child handles, scalars) and object slots (strings, node lists); 160 generated field accessors, `Is{Class}` tests and `ForEachChild` over handles (`tools/NeverSharp.Generate/emit-optimized.mjs`). `lookAhead`/failed `tryParse` truncate the store to a mark. Lists stay `List<Node>` (O7), texts stay strings (O5).
- **Gate (orchestrator rerun):** Track O passes every gate (50,177/50,177 in G1a, G1 ParseNone, Gate 1); Track F still passes. The tool diff is only the Track O traversal in `AstDump.DumpUnit` (reviewed line by line); the driver and benchmarks needed no change.
- **Measurement (development, not a protocol result):** `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` (ParseForTypeErrors, NativeAOT, BenchmarkDotNet): Track F 2,495 ms [2,485, 2,535], 737.8 MB, GCs 85/71/4; Track O after O6a 1,649 ms [1,635, 1,659], 823.5 MB, GCs 66/34/3. About 34 % faster with half the gen1 collections, although it allocates 12 % more (store over-capacity and growth garbage, delegates for method groups that gained a store parameter): the bytes now die young. Raw reports: `results/track-o/O6a/`.
- **Harness fix found on the way:** BenchmarkDotNet's NativeAOT build does not pass `-p:NeverSharpTrack` on; a first Track O measurement had silently measured Track F (identical allocation exposed it). The track is now set as an environment variable (documented in the benchmark project).
- Open for O6b (from the attempt): shrink the 40-byte record (rare fields to side tables), the mostly-null `JsDoc` object slot of 69 container kinds, initial sizing and growth (about 1.9× over-capacity), offset lookup per access, pooling the store.

### O6b: node arena layout (3 variants)

Brief: [modules/O6b.md](modules/O6b.md). Three attempts in parallel (Opus 5.5, high effort, no sub-agents), each changing only the storage layer (`Arena/`, `NodeStore.g.cs`, `emit-optimized.mjs`); every one passed the full Track O gate on the orchestrator's rerun (50,177/50,177 in every comparison). Branches `attempts/O6b/A..C`.

| Variant | Layout (from the reports) | Median, tuning corpus, cs-o-aot-ws (20 runs) | Allocated | GC pause | Peak working set | Rank |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| O6a (reference) | 40-byte record + slot blocks | 1,312 ms | 961 MB | 843 ms | 598 MiB | — |
| **A: compact records** | variable-length records in one `int[]` (header 5 ints + inline fields), object slots in a second array, rare and never-set fields in a side table, pooled scratch arrays copied to exact size (pinned) at the end of the parse | **1,093 ms** | **695 MB** | 679 ms | **450 MiB** | **1, merged** |
| B: structure of arrays | dense columns per base field (26 B/node), sparse columns for rare fields, pooled 32K-node segments | 1,137 ms | 848 MB | 667 ms | 487 MiB | 2 |
| C: header + payload arenas | 20-byte header (pinned) + typed payload structs in 17 shape arenas | 1,256 ms | 740 MB | 982 ms | 467 MiB | 3 |

Ranking as the brief fixes it (parse-only harness on the tuning corpus decides; all four runs rig-compliant, idle 3.5–9.6 %; 95 % percentile bootstrap): A/B 0.961 [0.946, 0.986], A/C 0.870 [0.857, 0.892], A/O6a 0.833 [0.815, 0.854]. The intervals do not overlap, so the median decides and the byte tie-break was not needed; A also allocates the least. Raw runs: `results/track-o/O6b/`.

- **Tie-break measurement failed and was not needed:** BenchmarkDotNet found several projects named NeverSharp.Benchmarks (the copies in the variant worktrees) and returned no result; after merging and removing the worktrees, the merged state measured `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` 1.456 s, 457.6 MB (O6a: 1,649 ms, 823.5 MB) — `results/track-o/O6b/merged-bdn/`.
- **Harness fix during the step:** the benchmark project compiled Track O without the NoAlloc attribute types (Track O's Gate 2 injects them through the analyzer); variants B and C removed their annotations for that reason, A kept them and was measurable after the fix (39677ae). Annotations do not change speed.
- A uses `[MethodImpl(AggressiveInlining)]` on generated accessors (allowed in Track O, not in Track F) and the first `[NoAlloc]` annotations: 12 of 2,150 methods (`noalloc-report.mjs`).
- From the reports: GC pause is about 60 % of the parallel wall time and depends on object count and references outside the store (node lists, strings), which points to O7 (lists) and O5 (text spans) next.

### O7a: node lists as handles

Brief: [modules/O7a.md](modules/O7a.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, no sub-agents; call sites converted with rewrite scripts), about 22 of 100 gate runs; commit f48ed75 (branch `attempts/O7a`), merged. Both gates pass on the orchestrator's rerun (Track O 50,177/50,177 everywhere; Track F unchanged); no tool diff.

- **Design:** `readonly struct NodeList` (header index; 0 = undefined); 24-byte headers (start, count, pos, end, transform flags, trailing-comma and missing-list bits); all finished lists' elements in one `Node[]`; lists under construction as frames of a reusable scratch stack (`ListBuilder`), copied at `createNodeArray`; the store's mark covers lists, elements, frames and scratch; arrays rented from the shared pool during the parse and moved to exact-size pinned arrays at the end. The one interleaved case (JSDoc comment parts and tags) finishes its tags list first; creating a list creates no node, so node order is unchanged.
- **Measurement (development, both runs rig-compliant, idle 5.8 % / 6.2 %):** tuning corpus, cs-o-aot-ws, 20 runs: **O7a 845 ms** vs main (O6b A) 1,100 ms, ratio 0.768 [0.760, 0.774]; allocated 679 vs 693 MB; GC pause 576 vs 695 ms; gen1 collections 23 vs 30; peak working set 415 vs 448 MiB. `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` after the merge: 1.206 s, 417.0 MB (before: 1.456 s, 457.6 MB). Raw: `results/track-o/O7a/`.
- NoAlloc: 27 of 2,193 methods annotated (before: 12), all in `Arena/`.
- Seen by the attempt, for later steps: per-call closures (`Core.Some(..., a0 => ...)` about 15 MB, one parser closure about 26 MB) and about 24 MB of boxed `Node`; O7b candidates: smaller list headers, small-list inline storage, per-list pooled arrays.

### O5a: text as spans, identifier interning

Brief: [modules/O5a.md](modules/O5a.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, no sub-agents), about 16 of 100 gate runs; commit 7b35566 (branch `attempts/O5a`), merged. Both gates pass on the orchestrator's rerun; no tool diff.

- **Design:** `TextRef` (two ints: start, length + 1; negative start = character-arena offset; default = undefined) for every text value; a per-parse `TextTable` holds the character arena (escapes, normalized numbers, cooked template text, joined JSDoc comment text, identifier escapes) and the interning table (span-keyed hash table, FNV-1a, chains newest-first — the equivalent of the brief's dictionary with span alternate lookup without a string per key); the scanner produces token values as handles (`GetTokenValue()` still returns TypeScript's string for the G1a dump); constant comparisons on spans; text fields in two int slots; truncation covers arena fill and interned count; pooled during the parse, sealed to exact arrays at the end. Strings remain only for tools/tests, the regex checker's named groups, diagnostic arguments on error paths, pragma arguments of known pragmas, rare numeric forms and constant factory texts.
- **Differences from TypeScript, not observable in the gates:** interned entries from rolled-back speculation are removed (TypeScript's map keeps them); `createIdentifier` skips computing the discarded `originalKeywordKind`.
- **Measurement (development, both rig-compliant, idle 7.6 % / 7.9 %):** tuning corpus, cs-o-aot-ws, 20 runs: **O5a 579 ms** vs main (O7a) 837 ms, ratio 0.692 [0.674, 0.705]; allocated 579 vs 660 MB; GC pause 347 vs 433 ms; peak working set 366 vs 418 MiB. `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` after the merge: 959 ms, 276.1 MB (before: 1.206 s, 417.0 MB). Raw: `results/track-o/O5a/`.
- NoAlloc: 39 of 2,251 methods (before: 27).
- Remaining allocations (from the attempt's profile): exact copies of the node record array (~100 MB), `CommentRange` objects (~40 MB), file bytes (~39 MB, step O1), closures (~24 MB), boxed `Node` (~19 MB), `Func` delegates (~12 MB). O5b candidates: keyword lookup (switch / perfect hash), open-addressing intern table, one-int text handle.

### O-NA: remaining allocations, NoAlloc coverage

Brief: [modules/ONA.md](modules/ONA.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, no sub-agents; annotation to a fixpoint with a scratch tool that compiles with the NoAlloc analyzer and removes failing markers), 12 of 60 gate runs; commit 8ca177b (branch `attempts/O-NA`), merged. Both gates pass on the orchestrator's rerun.

- **Allocations removed:** `CommentRange` as a struct with an iterator (~40 MB → 0); parser and scanner callbacks as struct callbacks (`IParserCallback<T>`, `IScannerCallback<T>`), a struct-visitor `ForEachChild`, loops instead of lambdas (closures ~31 MB and `Func` ~12 MB → about 0.5 MB of per-file setup); factory parameters typed `Node` instead of `object` (boxing 18 MB → 0); escapes and literal values into the character arena, hex/binary/octal values without strings up to 2^53. Not removed: the end-of-parse exact copies (~132 MB, they are the per-file result), file reading (step O1), per-file setup objects, pool warm-up.
- **NoAlloc:** 2,119 of 2,437 methods annotated (87 %; before 39), 0 with lifted constraints, 55 `[NoAllocUnchecked]` (arena growth 17, per-file result 8, error paths 24, regex checking 2, rare numeric forms 5 … listed by `noalloc-report.mjs`). The scanner's scan functions and the whole grammar are annotated; generated accessors carry markers the analyzer does not check (generated code).
- **Amendment prereg/v6 (decided by the author):** the attempt added a second allowlist (24 non-allocating runtime members; mirror entries of the 55 exceptions so that annotated callers keep their contract). It widens what Track O's Gate 2 accepts, so it became an amendment: the file moved to `eng/optimized.noalloc-allowlist.toml` (ruleset, wired in `eng/Gate2.Optimized.props`), the orchestrator reviewed all 24 runtime entries, and the report counts allowlist entries. Speed does not depend on it.
- **Measurement (development, both rig-compliant, idle 4.4 % / 7.5 %):** tuning corpus, cs-o-aot-ws: **O-NA 473 ms** vs main (O5a) 581 ms, ratio 0.815 [0.800, 0.833]; allocated 403 vs 529 MB; GC pause 114 vs 197 ms; peak working set 337 vs 368 MiB. Raw: `results/track-o/ONA/`.

### O1: file reading and decoding (A seam, B, C)

Brief: [modules/O1.md](modules/O1.md). The seam (commit 49dcb44, no attempt) moved reading into Track O (`IO/SourceText.cs`, `Parser.ParseFile`) and added the reader check (`tools/NeverSharp.ReaderCheck`, a gate step of `--track optimized`: `SourceText.Read` against the sys.ts reference decode on every file list and equivalence unit, 100,955 files). Variant A is that seam (`File.ReadAllBytes` + `Encoding.GetString`, one `byte[]` and one `string` per file). Two attempts in parallel (Opus 5.5, high effort, no sub-agents, own worktrees); both passed the full Track O gate including the reader check on the orchestrator's rerun, and Track F's gate. Branches `attempts/O1/B` (3130bc0), `attempts/O1/C` (1e50f84).

- **B: pooled bytes, char arena:** `RandomAccess.Read` into a per-thread byte buffer (`ArrayPool` dropped: it re-allocated large buffers after trimming), SIMD `Utf8.ToUtf16` with replacement into an exact-length uninitialized `char[]` on the pinned object heap (UTF-16 BOMs: copy or byte-swap, `Encoding.Unicode` only when surrogates occur); `char[]` replaces `string` in scanner, text table, parser and `SourceFile.text`; static helpers on `ReadOnlySpan<char>`. 2 gate runs.
- **C: native text:** `RandomAccess.Read` into a per-thread native buffer (memory-mapped files measured 2.5× slower on the many small files of the corpus and were dropped), SIMD `Utf8.ToUtf16` into `NativeMemory.Alloc`ed UTF-16 (UTF-16 BOMs through `Encoding.Unicode`/`BigEndianUnicode.GetChars`, which keeps the reference's replacement of lone surrogates); `NativeText` (owner: native memory, or a pinned string for `CreateSourceFile(fileName, string)`) and `SourceChars` (`char*` + length) replace `string` in scanner, text table and parser; the result owns the text (`SourceFile.Release()`, finalizer as a backstop); `SourceText.Read(path).Chars` keeps the tool contract with a managed `char[]`. Contract: whoever holds a span of a result's text keeps the result reachable while using it. 1 gate run. NoAlloc 2,121 of 2,453, the same 55 exceptions.
- **Ranking (all three runs rig-compliant, idle 4.3 % / 4.9 % / 3.0 %; tuning corpus, cs-o-aot-ws, 20 runs, 95 % percentile bootstrap):** A 481.3 ms, B 460.6 ms, **C 449.8 ms**; B/A 0.957 [0.932, 0.973], C/A 0.935 [0.917, 0.956], C/B 0.977 [0.962, 1.003]. C and B are not separated by the interval, so the byte tie-break decides: allocated A 405 MB, B 392 MB, **C 291 MB**; GC pause 113 / 95 / 71 ms; gen0 17 / 11 / 12; peak working set 338 / 337 / 351 MiB. **C merged** (fast-forward). Raw: `results/track-o/O1/` (`rank.mjs` computes the table).
- **After the merge:** `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` (BenchmarkDotNet, NativeAOT; it parses from strings, so it measures the text-type change, not reading) 814.8 ms, 160.9 MB (`results/track-o/O1/merged-bdn/`). The benchmark project needed `AllowUnsafeBlocks` for Track O.

### Track O endpoint (2026-10-22, Windows only)

Own protocol-compliant run (pre-registration §8), harness commit 51f5036, label `track-o-endpoint-run1`: 14 configurations (tsc6-parse, tsgo7-parse(-single), tsrs-parse(-single), cs-aot-ws, all eight cs-o-*) on the five benchmark repositories; rig checks ok, variance gate PASS, crosscheck A6 0.77–1.03. **Optimized track: G = 0.60 [0.59, 0.61]** (cs-o-aot-ws vs tsgo7-parse): upper bound < 1.00, so Track O parses faster than typescript-go 7.0.2 (disclosed: ParseForTypeErrors vs typescript-go's eager JSDoc). Secondary: single-threaded 0.920 [0.918, 0.922] vs tsgo7-parse-single; vs tsrs 1.52 [1.50, 1.54] (more than 20 % slower than tsrs, secondary); Track O / Track F in the same run 0.095. Results and verdict: `results/track-o/endpoint/2026-10-22T18-55-52-105Z/VERDICT.md`.

### O9: per-file overhead (A main, B, C; after the Track O endpoint)

Brief: [modules/O9.md](modules/O9.md). Added after the endpoint to close part of the gap to tsrs; the probe `results/track-o/probes/io/NOTE.md` showed that gap is parsing CPU, not I/O or scheduling. Two attempts in parallel (Opus 5.5, high effort, own worktrees); both passed the full Track O gate on the orchestrator's rerun and Track F's gate. Branches `attempts/O9/B` (ebd3f5f), `attempts/O9/C` (202dc86). Common part in both: per-thread parser cache instead of `ConcurrentBag`, per-thread interning hash table cleared only in the used buckets.

- **B: hand over the scratch:** results keep the arrays they were built in (trim only above 2× and 4 KB spare). The attempt itself found it slower: the spare pinned capacity results keep costs more than the copy saved; sizing from the text length or from recent files was worse (30–60 % more pinned bytes). 1 gate run.
- **C: per-thread slabs** (`Arena/Slab.cs`): records, list headers, list elements and arena characters written into per-thread slabs (64 KB doubling to full size; pinned managed below 256 KB, native from 256 KB, chosen by measurement: all-native 1.004, all-pinned 1.018 of the hybrid); handles are offsets from the store's range start; `NodeStore.Commit()` shrinks each range at the end of the parse, the next parse continues after it; slabs count their holders (thread, stores, texts) and are freed at zero, finalizer as backstop; source text decoded straight into the thread's character slab. The tree must not be read after `SourceFile.Release()` (nothing in the repo calls it). Gate run 1 failed on unit 046988 with OutOfMemoryException (dump tool: one thread per unit, full-size native slabs invisible to the GC); fixed by the small managed first slabs; run 2 passed. 2 gate runs. NoAlloc 2,118 of 2,466, the same 55 exceptions.
- **Ranking (all three rig-compliant, idle 6.3 / 3.2 / 7.1 %; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** cs-o-aot-ws A 443.8 ms, B 455.5 ms, **C 407.5 ms**; B/A 1.026 [0.996, 1.051], **C/A 0.918 [0.893, 0.936]**, C/B 0.895 [0.876, 0.910]. Single-threaded: A 2,840 ms, B 2,809 ms, C 2,651 ms; C/A 0.933 [0.927, 0.940]. Allocated (parallel) A 290 MB, B 246 MB, C 97 MB; peak working set 353 / 328 / 303 MiB. **C merged** (fast-forward). Raw: `results/track-o/O9/` (`rank.mjs`).
- **After the merge:** `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` 750.8 ms, 40.7 MB (after O1: 814.8 ms, 160.9 MB). `results/track-o/O9/merged-bdn/`.

### O10 (side table) and O23 (scanner: O2 + O3), run in parallel after O9

Briefs: [modules/O10.md](modules/O10.md), [modules/O23.md](modules/O23.md); chosen after the profile in [track-o-profile.md](track-o-profile.md) (last section). Three attempts in parallel (Opus 5.5, high effort, own worktrees; O10 touched `Arena/`, O23 only `Scanner/`); all passed the full Track O gate on the orchestrator's rerun, and Track F's gate. Branches `attempts/O10` (59c1e98), `attempts/O23/B` (2c7d65e), `attempts/O23/C` (5fe8e5b), rebased onto O10 as `attempts/O23/B-on-O10` (3023077), `attempts/O23/C-on-O10` (ca50726), both gated again.

- **O10 (no variants):** the side table as an append-only entry log with a linear-probing index in a per-thread slab kind (`SlabKind.Side`), owned by the result like the records; files without side entries cost nothing (21,180 of 24,511 tuning files). Placement unchanged: on the tuning corpus every field of a larger class is under 3 % (FunctionExpression.asteriskToken 6.6 % and PropertyDeclaration.exclamationToken 3.5 % on small classes). 3 gate runs. The 6 % `AddSide` share of the JIT profile did not show as wall-clock: EventPipe over-represents GC polls.
- **O23 B: lookup tables (scalar):** 128-entry ASCII flag and token tables (`Scanner.CharTables.cs`), `Scan` as a small method that skips ASCII trivia and handles ASCII identifiers and single-character punctuators before the unchanged scanner.ts loop; identifier, comment and string loops on the table; keyword lookup by a perfect hash built from `textToKeywordObj` (part of step O5b, evaluated here). 1 gate run.
- **O23 C: SIMD:** `Vector128` (NativeAOT's default x86-64-v2 target has no hardware Vector256) for identifier runs, whitespace after line breaks, line-comment and two-pass block-comment bodies, string and template bodies; the same kind of keyword perfect hash. 2 gate runs (one header fix).
- **Run 1 (all rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** main 414.7 ms, O10 409.4 ms, B 395.2 ms, C 397.3 ms; O10/main 0.987 [0.964, 1.010] (not slower: **O10 merged**); B/main 0.953 [0.935, 0.969], C/main 0.958 [0.940, 0.981], C/B 1.005 [0.987, 1.033] (overlap). Bytes in the parallel run C 84.0 vs B 92.5 MB, single-threaded equal (55.634 vs 55.641 MB); single-threaded B 2,553 ms, C 2,598 ms (C/B 1.018 [1.011, 1.024]). The parallel byte difference is within the run-to-run spread of the slabs, so the author chose to repeat the B/C ranking on top of O10 and decide on the repeat.
- **Run 2 (deciding; on O10, all rig-compliant):** O10 414.3 ms, **B 393.2 ms**, C 398.3 ms; B/O10 0.949 [0.911, 0.977], C/O10 0.961 [0.932, 0.981], C/B 1.013 [0.983, 1.050] (overlap again); bytes allocated: parallel B 96.9 vs C 99.5 MB, single-threaded B 53.753 vs C 53.763 MB, so the tie-break picks **B, merged**. Single-threaded B 2,570 ms, C 2,593 ms. Raw: `results/track-o/O10-O23/run1`, `run2` (`rank.mjs`).
- **After both merges:** `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` 639.1 ms, 38.9 MB (after O9: 750.8 ms, 40.7 MB). `results/track-o/O10-O23/merged-bdn/`.

### O5b (interning lookup) and O11 (speculative parsing), run in parallel after the additional run

Briefs: [modules/O5b.md](modules/O5b.md), [modules/O11.md](modules/O11.md); chosen from the profile after O23 (`artifacts/profile/o23-vscode4-cpu.speedscope.json`: speculation 9.9 % of all samples inclusive, `TextTable.Intern` 2.3 %). Three attempts in parallel (Opus 5.5, high effort, own worktrees); all passed the full Track O gate on the orchestrator's rerun, and Track F's gate. Branches `attempts/O5b` (bd83505), `attempts/O11/B` (4b215c3), `attempts/O11/C` (6265e8d), `attempts/O11/C-on-O5b` (db0fed1, gated again).

- **O5b (one attempt):** `TextTable.Hash` over 8-byte words without a loop up to 16 characters, word-wise match confirmation (`SameChars`); entries, order and handles unchanged. Interning microbenchmark 57–60 → 43 ms. A hash carried from the scanner was evaluated and dropped (the scanner also rescans speculated tokens). 2 gate runs. Only `Arena/Text.cs`.
- **O11 B: token replay:** the last token scanned in a rolled-back speculation is kept and handed out when `Scan` starts at its position again; exact invalidation by a serial bumped by every re-scan, mode or context change. Hit rate 56–60 % of rescanned tokens, but rescans are about 8 % of tokens: about 0.6 % possible, eaten by bookkeeping. Two earlier designs (per-scan chain buffer; budget from an environment variable) were slower. 4 gate runs.
- **O11 C: exact pre-decisions:** decided failures skip a lookahead or tryParse whose rollback would restore everything it changed, decided successes call the callback directly; token-only decisions (e.g. `tryParse(parseTypeArgumentsInExpression)` when the token is neither `<` nor `<<`: 1.73 M of 2.23 M speculations on the tuning corpus) and decisions on the next token through `Scanner.PeekPlainToken()` (only when the next token follows spaces/tabs only and is a plain identifier, keyword or simple punctuator); fallback to the real speculation otherwise. Speculations 2,234,037 → 130,591, characters scanned speculatively 3.27 M → 1.71 M; node and identifier counters stay exact (speculation never restored them). Async-arrow and parenthesized-arrow speculations left alone on purpose. 4 gate runs.
- **Run 1 (all rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** main 401.1 ms, O5b 398.2 ms, B 405.5 ms, **C 386.9 ms**; O5b/main 0.993 [0.950, 1.019] (not slower: **O5b merged**); B/main 1.011 [0.978, 1.044] (not better: not merged); **C/main 0.965 [0.935, 0.998]**, C/B 0.954 [0.931, 0.984]. Single-threaded: C/main 0.978 [0.972, 0.983].
- **Run 2 (C rebased onto O5b):** O5b 415.6 ms, C 392.8 ms, C/O5b **0.945 [0.924, 0.970]**; single-threaded 0.976 [0.971, 0.981]. **C merged.** Raw: `results/track-o/O5b-O11/run1`, `run2` (`rank.mjs`).
- **After both merges:** `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` 598.9 ms, 38.9 MB (after O23: 639.1 ms). `results/track-o/O5b-O11/merged-bdn/`.

### O12 (node creation) and O24 (scanner, second round), run in parallel after the second additional run

Briefs: [modules/O12.md](modules/O12.md), [modules/O24.md](modules/O24.md); chosen from the profile after O11 ([track-o-profile.md](track-o-profile.md): outside file reading, scanner 26 %, node factory 20 %). Two single attempts in parallel (Opus 5.5, high effort, own worktrees); both passed the full Track O gate on the orchestrator's rerun, and Track F's gate. Branches `attempts/O12` (84ce783), `attempts/O24` (24d144a), `attempts/O24-on-O12` (836eac7, gated again).

- **O12:** the generator emits `NodeStore.New{Class}(kind?, transformFlags, fields…)` for 53 hot classes, writing the whole record once at constant offsets (side fields only when set); the hot factory functions compute fields and transform flags from their arguments and call it; `CreateNode` / `Finish` replace `Create` plus setters; `getTransformFlagsSubtreeExclusions` as a generated per-kind table (computed by running TypeScript's own function on every kind; 0 differences over 359 kinds against the old switch); direct calls instead of interfaces. Creation order and node counts unchanged. 2 gate runs.
- **O24:** O23 C's SIMD parts ported on top of the lookup-table scanner where they measurably win (identifier runs 16/8 characters per block, template, line-comment, string and block-comment bodies, vector keyword compare, white space after line breaks), `Scan` bookkeeping in locals, a comment-directive pre-check, simple decimal integers without `ScanNumber`. Scan-only 119 → 82 ms, in-memory parse 595 → 547 ms in the attempt's microbenchmarks. 2 gate runs.
- **Run 1 (all rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** main 399.3 ms, **O12 379.6 ms**, O24 389.9 ms; O12/main **0.951 [0.929, 0.981]** (single-threaded 0.956 [0.945, 0.966]) — **O12 merged**; O24/main 0.976 [0.940, 1.010] (single-threaded 1.004), not slower.
- **O24 rebased onto O12:** run 2 not rig-compliant (idle before run 13.9 %; archived as `run2-not-compliant`, O24/O12 0.991, single-threaded 0.967); run 3 rig-compliant (idle 11.4 %): O12 376.8 ms, O24 377.5 ms, O24/O12 1.002 [0.980, 1.029], single-threaded 0.989 [0.977, 1.000]. Not slower, so **O24 merged** by the brief's rule; on top of O12 its gain is not measurable in the parse-only harness (the in-memory benchmark below shows it).
- **After both merges:** `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` 485.4 ms, 38.9 MB (after O11: 598.9 ms). `results/track-o/O12-O24/` (`run1`, `run2-not-compliant`, `run3`, `merged-bdn`, `rank.mjs`).

### O25 (expression descent) and O26 (token consumption), run in parallel after the third additional run

Briefs: [modules/O25.md](modules/O25.md), [modules/O26.md](modules/O26.md); chosen from the profile after O24. Two single attempts in parallel (Opus 5.5, high effort, own worktrees); both passed the full Track O gate on the orchestrator's rerun, and Track F's gate. Branches `attempts/O25` (65b3d74), `attempts/O26` (2b721c3), `attempts/O26-on-O25` (98c50b0, gated again).

- **O25:** only `Parser.Expressions.cs`: a direct path to the primary expression for tokens only `ParsePrimaryExpression` acts on (`IsPlainPrimaryStart`), an early return after an operand when the next token continues no level (`ContinuesOperand`, a table computed from `GetBinaryOperatorPrecedence` and `IsAssignmentOperator`), the redundant second `ParseMemberExpressionRest` call at the start of `ParseCallExpressionRest` dropped except on `?.`, `<`, `<<`, an exit at the top of `ParseMemberExpressionRest`, binary precedence from a table. Each shortcut argued exact in the attempt's report; node and identifier counts unchanged. In-memory parse about −10 % in the attempt's benchmark. 2 gate runs.
- **O26:** `Parser.State.cs` and `Scanner.Instance.cs`: NativeAOT did not inline `Utilities.IsKeyword`, so `NextToken` is inlined with the keyword test in place, `NextTokenAfter(kind)` where the caller has just compared the token (the test folds for constant punctuation), the parse helpers (`ParseExpected`, `ParseOptional`, …) inlined with their error branches out of line; `Scan` hands the first character and its class from the trivia loop to the dispatch. In-memory parse 0.968 [0.964, 0.971] in the attempt's interleaved benchmark. 1 gate run.
- **Run 1 (all rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** main 370.2 ms, O25 363.3 ms, O26 361.3 ms; **O25/main 0.981 [0.958, 0.992]** (single-threaded 0.967 [0.961, 0.979]), **O26/main 0.976 [0.951, 0.997]** (single-threaded 0.979). **O25 merged** (cherry-picked onto main, which had a documentation commit after the base).
- **Run 2 invalid (orchestrator's error):** the fast-forward merge of O25 had failed, so the "O26 on O25" build was O26 on main without O25 (O26/O25 1.000, single-threaded 1.019); archived as `run2-invalid-base`, not used.
- **Run 3 (O26 correctly rebased onto O25):** O25 363.1 ms, O26 360.9 ms, O26/O25 0.994 [0.978, 1.016], single-threaded 1.000 [0.990, 1.004]; not slower, so **O26 merged** by the brief's rule. On the tuning corpus file reading dominates, so both gains show mainly in the in-memory benchmark below.
- **After both merges:** `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` 430.1 ms, 38.9 MB (after O24: 485.4 ms). `results/track-o/O25-O26/`.

### O27 (identifiers) and O28 (call/member rest, lists), run in parallel after the fourth additional run

Briefs: [modules/O27.md](modules/O27.md), [modules/O28.md](modules/O28.md); chosen from the profile and I/O probe after O26 (`results/track-o/probes/io2/NOTE.md`: the gap to tsrs is parse user CPU, vscode single-threaded 1.79 s against 1.32 s). Two single attempts in parallel (Opus 5.5, high effort, own worktrees); both passed the full Track O gate on the orchestrator's rerun, and Track F's gate. Branches `attempts/O27` (7b6c8a8), `attempts/O28` (506eb14), `attempts/O27-on-O28` (4716e49, gated again).

- **O27:** only `Arena/Text.cs`: texts up to 16 characters (87 % of interns) keyed by their characters in two `Vector128<ushort>` (loaded directly where the NUL-terminated source allows, masked to the length), hash without a branch on the length, entries store the key so a lookup never reads the text; longer texts keep O5b's word hash; a parse starts with 256 buckets of the thread's array. Dropped after measurement: pre-interned keywords (2.8 % of interns), a direct-mapped cache, a hash carried from the scanner (upper bound 1.3 %), branchless insert-or-find, packed keys. Entries grow from 16 to 48 bytes: +1 MB allocated single-threaded. 3 gate runs.
- **O28:** `NodeStore.FinishNodeArray` copies list elements and computes the list's transform flags in one loop with one header write; the list functions inlined so the per-context switches fold at each call site; `ParseArgumentList` as a fixed-context loop; `.` first in `ParseMemberExpressionRest`, member-continuation checks before calls, `ParseCallExpressionRestAfterMemberRest` returns at once on tokens other than `?.` and `(`, `FinishChainNode` written out (NativeAOT did not inline `FinishNode`). Each shortcut argued exact in the attempt's report. 2 gate runs.
- **Run 1 (all rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** main 370.3 ms, O27 364.6 ms, **O28 356.1 ms**; O27/main 0.985 [0.958, 1.006] (single-threaded 0.999), **O28/main 0.962 [0.940, 0.976]** (single-threaded 0.974 [0.965, 0.987]). **O28 merged** (fast-forward, checked).
- **Run 2 (O27 rebased onto O28):** O28 366.0 ms, O27 362.3 ms, O27/O28 0.990 [0.956, 1.006], single-threaded 1.007 [0.998, 1.017]; not slower, so **O27 merged** by the brief's rule (its gain is within noise in the parse-only harness).
- **After both merges:** `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` 410.7 ms, 39.9 MB (after O26: 430.1 ms, 38.9 MB). `results/track-o/O27-O28/`.

### O29: NativeAOT inlining audit (after the fifth additional run)

Brief: [modules/O29.md](modules/O29.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, own worktree); passed the full Track O gate on the orchestrator's rerun, and Track F's gate. Branch `attempts/O29` (6df134f).

- **Method:** the published NativeAOT driver disassembled with Visual Studio's `dumpbin -disasm` (method names from the PDB); scratch scripts list every call and tail jump in the hot functions with the callee's size and diff call counts between builds. Examined: scanner, token consumption, the whole expression descent, statements, lists, types, declarations, node creation, side table.
- **Kept** (attempt's interleaved in-memory benchmark): O25's `ContinuesOperand`, `IsPlainPrimaryStart`, `BinaryOperatorPrecedence`, `ParsePlainPrimaryExpression` inlined (0.983); a `FinishNode(Node, int)` overload without the nullable end, which NativeAOT inlines at about 30 sites (0.996); `WithJSDoc` test in line, work out of line (0.992); `NodeFactory.Parenthesizer` memoized value in line (0.997). **Dropped:** `AggressiveInlining` on `FinishNode` everywhere (1.016), inline `FinishNode` copies, `ParseTokenNode`/`IsIdentifier`, `IsPropertyName`/`EscapeLeadingUnderscores`, context wrappers, yield/arrow pre-checks, small parse helpers (all 0.998–1.007). In-memory parse overall 0.969 [0.965, 0.970] in the attempt. 1 gate run.
- **Run 1 (rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** main 358.1 ms, O29 358.8 ms, O29/main 1.002 [0.972, 1.027], single-threaded 0.991 [0.982, 1.001]; not slower, so **O29 merged** (fast-forward, checked). File reading dominates the tuning corpus.
- **After the merge:** `M5.ParseTuningCorpus` 407.4 ms, 39.9 MB (after O28: 410.7 ms); smaller than the attempt's in-memory ratio (BenchmarkDotNet run-to-run spread was 3–7 % in earlier steps). `results/track-o/O29/`.

### B1: binder traversal, symbols, declarations (2026-10-24)

Brief: [modules/B1.md](modules/B1.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, medium effort); commit 414e803, merged. Ported into `src/NeverSharp.Optimized/Binder/`: `Binder.cs` (binder state, `bind`, `bindWorker`, containers and container chain, `declareSymbol` with merging, exclusions and its diagnostics, module/class/source-file members, module instance state, all declaration kinds, imports/exports, JSDoc class/param/property/overload/import tags), `Binder.Flow.cs` (the complete control-flow traversal: it had to be ported now, because unreachable code is bound in child order without hoisting and so changes `locals` order and the container chain), `Binder.Special.cs` (strict-mode and reserved-word checks; stubs for B3), `Utilities.Binder.cs` (about 60 helpers), a hash index over symbol-table entries, process-wide symbol ids for private names (renumbered in manifest order by the dump writer, as the oracle's counter accumulates across units). Gate B section `symbols`: **49,420 of 50,177 identical in both JSDoc modes**; all 757 differences come from B3 features (special assignments 330, of them 117 TS/TSX expando members; CommonJS module detection 64; combinations with `module.exports` 194; JSDoc typedefs 119 + 50 combined). `flow`: 119 differing units (JSDoc typedefs); `diagnostics`: 7. Not stored yet: `possiblyExhaustive`, `flowNodeWhenTrue`/`flowNodeWhenFalse` (not in the dump; the checker needs them later). 6 gate runs; parser gates not run (no parser code changed).

Amendment v8 (§16): measurement profiles full/quick/binder. **Binder endpoint** (profile binder, harness 7019057; rig ok, variance gate PASS): **G = 1.207 [1.191, 1.219]** cs-o-aot-ws-bind vs tsgo7-bind → **inconclusive** (contains 1.20); single-threaded 0.985 [0.983, 0.988]; the parallel gap is the binder's allocation (vscode 3,984 MB, 581 ms GC pause). `results/phase2/binder-endpoint/2026-10-24T11-21-53-808Z/VERDICT.md`.

### BO1: binder allocations (2026-10-24)

Brief: [modules/BO1.md](modules/BO1.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort); commit ab1f45e, merged; Gate B and the full Track O parser gate pass on the orchestrator's rerun. Cause confirmed: the binder's `parent` writes after commit went into the side table and grew a managed slab per file (2,497 MB on vscode), plus 40 MB of character-arena slabs for names added after `Seal`. Design: parents written after the parse in a dense per-file array (`handle >> 2`, a per-thread `SlabKind.Parents` slab; the parse-only path unchanged); committed ranges grow in place in the thread's slab when no parse runs (`SlabRange.OpenCommitted` / `GrowCommitted`); all `BinderStore` arrays as regions of one range in a per-thread `SlabKind.Binder` slab, sized from the node count, `node.flowNode` as a dense array; `SourceFile.Release()` also releases the binding. Allocation vscode `--bind`: 3,972 → 94 MB, GC pause 542 → 47 ms. **Run 1 (rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** cs-o-aot-ws-bind 892.1 → 461.1 ms, **0.517 [0.504, 0.526]**; single-threaded 0.835 [0.828, 0.838]; parse-only 0.978 [0.964, 1.001] (not slower). **Merged.** `results/phase2/BO1/`. NoAlloc 2,349 of 2,971 (binder 107 of 371), 55 exceptions.

### BO2: binder CPU hot paths (2026-10-24)

Brief: [modules/BO2.md](modules/BO2.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort); commit a7554cb, merged; Gate B passes on the orchestrator's rerun (only `Binder/` changed, no parser gate needed). Changes (attempt's in-memory benchmark, bind time): modifier flags computed from the modifier list instead of writing `modifierFlagsCache` into the side table (−7.6 %); per-identifier and per-node checks (contextual reserved words ruled out by their characters, `isExpression` skipped where always true, `jsDoc` read before `canHaveJSDoc`, `setParent` without reading first; −13 %); children of the most frequent node kinds bound directly instead of through delegates (−9 % and −4 %); `declareSymbol` hashes once. Rejected: a struct visitor with a child stack (+1.8 %), `SkipLocalsInit` (noise). The constructor's zeroing was an EventPipe artefact (removing it: noise). In-memory bind 496 → 336 ms. **Run 1 (rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** cs-o-aot-ws-bind 469.8 → 439.8 ms, **0.936 [0.921, 0.956]**; single-threaded 0.925 [0.918, 0.935]. **Merged.** `results/phase2/BO2/`.

### BO3: binder per-file setup and dispatch (2026-10-25)

Brief: [modules/BO3.md](modules/BO3.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort); commits 0ed9c2a, 9af4a27, 1652758, merged; Gate B passes on the orchestrator's rerun (only `Binder/` changed). Measured in NativeAOT first: page faults of fresh slab pages dominate the per-file setup (272 ns per page single-threaded, 530–660 ns with 12–24 threads faulting). Changes: small files (24,460 of 24,511 on the tuning corpus) bind into a reused per-thread scratch block and are copied into an exact-size committed range at the end (faults −15 %); larger initial capacities (grows 7,833 → 1,066); a token fast path in `Bind`; a length pre-check for contextual reserved words; compact maps copied as they are. Rejected: parents written by the parser (would change what the binder observes in `getModuleInstanceState`), a bitmap form of `node.flowNode` (slower), native first binder slabs (dump-tool risk), more direct dispatch (negligible). In-memory bind 0.939 in the attempt. **Run 1 (rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** cs-o-aot-ws-bind 440.5 → 435.3 ms, 0.988 [0.965, 1.019]; single-threaded 0.987 [0.983, 0.994]; peak working set single-threaded 515 → 456 MiB. Not slower: **merged**. `results/phase2/BO3/`.

### BO4: binder per-file memory handling, sentry (2026-10-25)

Brief: [modules/BO4.md](modules/BO4.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort); commit 6f014dc, merged; Gate B passes on the orchestrator's rerun (only `Binder/` changed). Sentry: 8,208 of 8,932 files are .tsx (average 5.1 KB), 0.18 symbols per node (JSX attributes; vscode 0.12), binder cost per node as on vscode; its gap to tsrs is mostly parallel (GC pauses follow surviving managed bytes per file). Changes: the module symbol's quoted name written into the text arena without strings (the largest GC lever), `localSymbol` in its own map, the hash index not committed (a later lookup scans the table; adding rebuilds it — a checker would rebuild it once), symbol records 48 → 40 B, `node.flowNode` slots per 5 record words, flow records 28 → 12 B, a smaller store object with rare fields created on demand. Page faults −10 to −11 %, binder managed allocation per round e.g. sentry 8.6 → 3.2 MB. Rejected: non-temporal copies, parents per 5 words, a faster table hash, native second binder slabs, larger object-slot arrays. **Run 1 (rig-compliant; tuning corpus, 20 runs):** cs-o-aot-ws-bind 427.0 → 422.0 ms, 0.988 [0.957, 1.029]; single-threaded 0.985 [0.977, 0.996]; peak working set single-threaded 456 → 418 MiB. Not slower: **merged**. `results/phase2/BO4/`.

### P1: printer (2026-10-25) — printer gate passes

Left out: print handlers and their parenthesizer substitutions, comment emission beyond `removeComments: true`, source maps, emit helpers and generated names (transformer-only). Printer gate (`gate.mjs --phase printer`; oracle `dump-ts-printer.mjs` = `createPrinter({ removeComments: true, newLine: LineFeed }).printFile`): **50,177 of 50,177 identical** (ParseForTypeErrors). 6 gate runs; no parser code changed.

### C0: checker oracle, program layer, checker skeleton (2026-10-25)

Brief: [modules/C0.md](modules/C0.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, medium effort); commit ce0fb21, cherry-picked onto main (conflicts with P1 in `gate.mjs` and the dump tool's `Program.cs` resolved by keeping both modes); the printer gate and Gate C rerun on main reproduce the attempt's results. Oracle `dump-ts-checker.mjs`: per unit a tsc 6.0.3 program of the unit plus the default library (`lib.es2025.full.d.ts`, 82 files; options `{ noResolve: true }`, effective defaults in the dump header: target ES2025, module ES2022, moduleResolution bundler, all strict-family options true, allowJs false), sections `diagnostics` (option/global/semantic/suggestion with related information), `types` and `symbols` (typeWriter.ts format); library diagnostics once in `<out>.lib.txt`; about 19 minutes per mode, deterministic over two runs and independent of unit order; 120 s timeout for two slow units; dumps about 3.6 GB per mode, cached in `artifacts/equivalence/dumps/ts-checker-types`. Track O: `Program/` (compiler options, host, default library parsed, line-mapped and bound once per process and shared read-only), `Checker/` skeleton (types and signatures as int handles in creation order, `SymbolRef` (file, index), `NodeRef` (file, node), links per kind; checkers never write into shared library files), layout in [phase3-checker-layout.md](phase3-checker-layout.md); `gate.mjs --phase checker [--sample N]`. **Gate C with the empty checker: 1,607 of 50,177 identical** (diagnostics 8,432, types 1,713, symbols 1,993), 0 crashed, library diagnostics identical. Open points: JavaScript units are not in the program without `allowJs` (only TS6504; Gate C would not exercise JS typing), the top-level-await reparse for files that are modules only by extension needs a parser seam, `@ts-ignore` filtering and options-diagnostic sorting are left for C8.

### C1: checker core (2026-10-25)

Brief: [modules/C1.md](modules/C1.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, medium effort); commits 516dae6, 01f04fa, 5afdcab, be8baf4, cherry-picked onto main; Gate C rerun on main reproduces the attempt's numbers. Ported: global merging (module augmentations, UMD globals), `resolveName` with its error and suggestion paths, alias and import/export resolution under `noResolve`, intrinsic/literal/union/simplified intersection types, type references and global types, declared types of classes/interfaces/aliases/enums/type parameters, base types, member/property/signature resolution, variable-like declaration types; an expression subset (property and element access, calls with the first signature and explicit type arguments, await/yield, import.meta, approximate contextual typing); unused identifiers, JSX basics, property initialization, override modifiers, parameter decorators, implicit-any returns, comment directives (`@ts-ignore`/`@ts-expect-error`, 2578); symbol chains and type/signature text with node-reuse underlines. Transition code: an "uncertain" mechanism suppresses diagnostics derived from approximated types — to be removed entirely by the end of C6. Stubs `TODO(C2…C9)`. Layout additions in `TypeStore.cs` (transient symbol members/exports, `SymTable`, object/type-parameter/union data, index infos). **Gate C (full corpus): 21,495 of 50,177 identical** (diagnostics 40,603, types 23,029, symbols 40,990), 0 crashed. 4 gate runs.

Then: JavaScript/JSX units checked with `allowJs: true` (author's decision, §17), oracle regenerated.

### allowJs in Gate C and C9a: syntactic diagnostics of JavaScript files (2026-10-25)

`allowJs: true` for JavaScript/JSX units (commit bf768e0; oracle regenerated, cached in the main checkout): Gate C 20,196 identical, all 1,299 JS/JSX units crashed (a C0 stub for JavaScript syntactic diagnostics). C9a (brief [modules/C9a.md](modules/C9a.md); Opus 5.5, medium effort; commit 47361f7, merged; rerun on main reproduces it): `getJSSyntacticDiagnosticsForFile` (`Program/JsSyntacticDiagnostics.cs`), the emit-path check of `verifyCompilerOptions` (TS5055/5056: outputs next to their inputs), the JavaScript part of `getBindAndCheckDiagnosticsForFileNoCache` (plain JS keeps only the `plainJSErrors` codes, checked JS adds JSDoc diagnostics). **Gate C: 20,666 of 50,177 identical, 0 crashed** (diagnostics 40,512, types 22,273, symbols 40,527); TS units unchanged, 470 of 1,299 JS units identical. Left for C9: JS-specific uses for unused-identifier checks, `this`/`module.exports`/`require`/`unique symbol` types in JS, plain-JS filtering of duplicate-declaration errors.

### C2: node builder and type printing (2026-10-25)

Brief: [modules/C2.md](modules/C2.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, medium effort), in parallel with C3 and C9a; commit 7feac39, cherry-picked onto main after C9a without conflicts; the printer gate (50,177/50,177) and Gate C pass/measure on main. Ported: `typeToString` / `signatureToString` / `symbolToString` / `typePredicateToString` and the baseline type text, `typeToTypeNodeHelper` for every type kind (kinds C3 has not created yet read their data through marked seams), anonymous/object type nodes, type references, signatures and type parameters with `T_1` renaming, fake scopes, symbol chains with import types, truncation and `formatUnionTypes`, the type parenthesizer rules, the syntactic builder (expressionToTypeNode.ts) and `tryReuseExistingTypeNode`, symbol accessibility; nodes built in a checker-owned node store (mark/truncate per print) and printed with P1's printer (an `IsFullySynthetic` hook and an underline writer for the baseline's underlines). C1's minimal printer removed. Known: 18 units print `T_1` where tsc prints `T`, because C1 does not instantiate inherited members/aliases/heritage arguments yet (C3). `Checker.NodeBuilder.Support.cs` holds stand-ins for C3 functions, to be replaced by C3's definitions. **Gate C on main (with C9a): 21,772 of 50,177 identical, 0 crashed** (diagnostics 40,517, types 23,291, symbols 40,851).

### C3: instantiation and generics, merged onto C2 (2026-10-25)

Brief: [modules/C3.md](modules/C3.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, medium effort), in parallel with C2 and C9a; commits 805bb47..e4997b9 on `attempts/C3` (alone, on main before C2/C9a: Gate C 21,330). Ported: type mappers and `instantiateType` with tsc's limits, `instantiateSignature`/`instantiateSymbol`, object type instantiations and their caching, generic type references and aliases, `this` types, tuples (labeled/optional/rest/variadic, normalization), array/readonly array types, `keyof`, indexed access, mapped and conditional types (distributive; the `infer` inference call left for C6), template literal and string mapping types, substitution and `unique symbol` types, late-bound members, constraints and defaults, `getApparentType`/`getReducedType`/`getBaseConstraintOfType`, instantiation expressions, import types, literal and `typeof` types in tsc's creation order. Merge (separate agent, Opus 5.5, medium effort; commits 266fc03 and 50c796d on `merge/C3`, cherry-picked onto main as 6fdd2fd and 7daf266; code tree identical to the measured one): conflicts in `Checker.Print.cs` and `Checker.TypeNodes.cs` resolved in favour of C2's node builder and C3's instantiation-aware defaults; C3's text printer (`Checker.PrintGenerics.cs`) removed; C2's stand-ins in `Checker.NodeBuilder.Support.cs`/`Checker.NodeBuilder.Reuse.cs` replaced by C3's definitions, four remaining node-builder functions for mapped/conditional types ported (they crashed 232 units in the first merged run). The merged build timed out on `recursiveConditionalCrash3.ts` (19 s vs 0.7 s): C2's `visitAndTransformType` lacked tsc's `serializedTypes` cache, now added (cache per outermost print rather than per declaration, since the builder's node store is reset per print). Remaining C2 stand-ins: `FunctionFlags`, `TypePredicateInfo`, `InstantiateTypePredicate`, `GetExpandedParameters`, `IsTypeIdenticalTo`, reverse-mapped accessors. Units with a spurious `T_1` (dump line equal to tsc's once `T_1` → `T`): 505 before, 140 after; the rest are uninstantiated call results (C6). Against main before the merge: 2 previously identical units differ (007655, 046906), and 25/3/6 per-section losses that C3 alone shows too (call inference, circularity). Printer gate 50,177/50,177. **Gate C: 23,328 of 50,177 identical, 0 crashed** (diagnostics 40,668, types 25,008, symbols 41,507; before: 21,772 / 40,517 / 23,291 / 40,851).

### C4: type relations (2026-10-25)

the fake scope counts as the enclosing declaration, as TypeScript's Block; the builder's store is truncated only after prints that add nothing to the cache); `getTypePredicateOfSignature` without a resolving marker (the 2456 of an assertion call's effects signature); Gate C does not pass: 001971 differs in the names generated for shadowed type parameters (`O1_1` etc.; tsc's `typeParameterShadowsOtherTypeParameterInScope` finds a different type parameter through its fake scope chain where the C# one does not, probably the scope entered for type parameters of reused existing signature nodes), and 027977 times out under the gate's load (a harness and load question; timeouts unchanged). 6 gate runs (2 full incl. the baseline, 4 with `--sample 2000`); probes of ≤ 13 units of own lists not counted.

Follow-up by the orchestrator (2026-10-29, commit 160d746): 001971's names of shadowed type parameters come from TypeScript's `pushFakeScope` undo, `forEach(newLocals, s => locals.delete(s))` and `forEach(oldLocals, s => locals.set(...))`: core.ts `forEach` stops at the first truthy result, so only the first name added (and the first replaced) is undone and the other type parameters of a signature stay in the reused fake scope, where `typeParameterShadowsOtherTypeParameterInScope` of the next overload finds them (`R1` is removed, `O1` stays, hence `R1, O1_1, …`; found by instrumenting a copy of the oracle's typescript.js). Ported as written (records new and replaced names, undoes as `forEach` does; `SymTable.Delete` added); the C# kept a copy of the table before instead. Full run after the change: **Gate C 50,176 of 50,177 identical** (diagnostics, types and symbols 50,176 each), 0 differing, 1 crashed: 027977 (timeout under the gate's load, outcome tsc ok, C# timeout; identical alone, 22.6 s together with 001971). Gate C passes except for that timeout; whether it is accepted is the author's decision.

Author decision (2026-10-29): 027977's timeout under the gate's load is accepted (identical when run alone); **Gate C counts as passed** at 160d746 (50,176 of 50,177 identical, 0 differing).

### C11: module interop of files, the last repository differences, tsrs check (2026-10-29)

Brief: [modules/C11.md](modules/C11.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, medium effort), alone, directly on main; commits 6e82f2d, f5b5d1d, 877a4c9 (and this entry). **Module interop port** (checker.ts 6.0.3, structure as checker.go): `resolveESModuleSymbol` (2497, the synthetic-default and `default`-property module types of namespace imports and import calls) with its callers `getTargetOfNamespaceImport`, `getTargetOfNamespaceExport`, `getExternalModuleMember` (now in full: `symbolFromVariable` of `export =` modules, `getPropertyOfVariable`, `combineValueAndTypeSymbols`, the synthetic default) and `checkImportCallExpression`; `getTargetofModuleDefault` in full (`resolveExportByName`, `isSyntacticDefault`, `canHaveSyntheticDefault`, `isOnlyImportableAsDefault`, `reportNonDefaultExport`, the 1259 path), `getTargetOfImportSpecifier` (the `default` specifier), `getModuleSpecifierForImportOrExport`; `cloneTypeAsModuleType` with typescript-go's `ExportTypeLinks` (`target`, `originatingImport`) and their readers (`invocationErrorRecovery` in `invocationError` and `resolveDecorator`, the related information of `checkTypeRelatedTo`); `getTypeWithSyntheticDefaultOnly`, `getTypeWithSyntheticDefaultImportType` (caches by type, as typescript-go's `cachedTypes`), `createDefaultPropertyWrapperForModule`. `errorNoModuleMemberSymbol` now tests the module symbol's `default` export, as checker.ts. Left out (throw where reachable): the node16/nodenext branches (JSON default-only imports, `module.exports` of ES modules, CommonJS synthetic defaults) and the project-reference branch of `canHaveSyntheticDefault`: unreachable with module ES2022 and without project references. `Program.GetEmitSyntaxForUsageLocation` exposes program.ts's worker to the checker. Repros (scratch: `export =` of functions, classes, objects and non-module aliases; namespace, default, named and `default as` imports; `export * as`; `.mts`/`.cts` files; `__esModule` and syntactic defaults in declaration files; import calls; assignment errors that print the module types) identical with tsc 6.0.3; vscode's 46 missing 2497 fixed.

**tldraw and sentry: one cause.** `checkNodeDeferred` kept a single deferred-node list for the file being checked; checker.ts keeps `links.deferredNodes` per enclosing source file and drops nodes of files already type-checked. Checking `TldrawEditor.tsx` resolved a function expression of `useCanvasEvents.ts` (a later file), whose deferred body was then checked with `TldrawEditor.tsx` and created `"mouse"` before `TLUserPreferences.test.ts` created `"trackpad"` (found by instrumenting a scratch copy of the oracle's typescript.js in `getStringLiteralType` and a temporary C# probe, removed before committing); the same misplaced deferral checked sentry's `.map` callback before the `.filter` callback, so the union's cached iteration-type error (2488, reported once per union type) landed on the other binding pattern. Fixed by porting the per-file deferral and the TypeChecked flag; no sorting.

**Equivalence (tsc 6.0.3 vs C# `cs-o-aot-ws --check --single`, `check-equivalence.mjs`): all five repositories identical** — vscode 101,493, sentry 237,529, bluesky 33,337, playwright 30,808, tldraw 51,900 (0 / 0 differences each). typescript-go 7.0.2 single: 101,933 / 237,531 / 33,337 / 30,838 / 52,604; tsrs 0.18.0 single: 101,941 / 237,533 / 33,337 / 30,838 / 52,612.

**tsrs check driver** (`harness/bench/rust/parse-rs --check [--single]`): `tsrs_compiler::new_program(ProgramOptions::new(new_parsed_command_line(check_options, files, …), new_cached_fs_compiler_host(cwd, bundled::wrap_fs(osvfs::fs()), bundled::default_lib_path(), …)))`, then `get_semantic_diagnostics(ctx, None)` and `get_global_diagnostics(ctx)`; the options of the Go driver's `checkOptions`, explicitly; the program API's defaults otherwise (no leaf freeing, no checker recycling). Parallelism: tsrs's default pool (16 checkers on the 24-thread reference machine: half the parallelism but every core up to 16, 4–32, at most one per 32 checked files; locality assignment with work stealing); `--single`: `ProgramOptions.single_threaded`. Same JSON fields as the other check drivers. `bench.mjs`: `tsrs-check`, `tsrs-check-single`, added to profile `check` (binding rows unchanged); `build-rs.mjs` also validates `--check` on the empty list; `check-equivalence.mjs` reports tsrs's counts (`--skip-tsrs`); docs/benchmark-harness.md §4.1. Functional check (one run each, through peakmem; diagnostics, peak working set, wall): vscode 101,941, 2,747 MiB, 2.2 s / single 101,941, 2,162 MiB, 19.2 s; sentry 237,533, 998 MiB, 0.9 s / 237,533, 886 MiB, 8.1 s; bluesky 33,337, 258 MiB, 0.2 s / 33,337, 178 MiB, 1.3 s; playwright 30,838, 461 MiB, 0.3 s / 30,838, 328 MiB, 1.9 s; tldraw 52,612, 441 MiB, 0.4 s / 52,612, 309 MiB, 2.8 s; tuning (24,511 files) 137,331, 7,613 MiB, 9.2 s / 137,578, 2,588 MiB, 18.3 s.
