### C12: the checker's tuning program (2026-10-29/30)

Brief: [modules/C12.md](modules/C12.md). **First attempt (2026-10-29, Opus 5.5, nothing committed):** the whole tuning corpus (24,511 files) as one program is no valid workload: its units are global scripts whose same-named globals merge across units, and tsc 6.0.3 itself throws on that program; the C# crash the brief asked about (`SymbolFlagsOf` in `GetLateBoundSymbol`) was a faithful port of one of tsc's crashes (a.ts `function f() {}`, b.ts `function f() {}` / `f[Symbol.iterator] = function() {}`). **Author's decision (2026-10-30, commit 883d16c):** the checker measures on its own list `tuning-check` — the tuning corpus without the 7 units tsc cannot check in that program (`harness/bench/tuning-check-exclude.txt`, each with its crash; `node harness/bench/tuning-check.mjs` writes `artifacts/bench/filelists/tuning-check.txt`, 24,504 files); `tuning.txt` unchanged.

**Second attempt** (Opus 5.5, medium effort, alone, directly on main; commits d6946ff, def4237, e23cbdc, c138f59 and this entry). Start: tsc 6.0.3 135,770 diagnostics, C# `--check --single` 135,913, 61 files differing. Causes, each found by delta debugging the file list (scratch tools; the oracle's typescript.js instrumented in a scratch copy for `suggestionCount` and the failing name resolutions) and fixed by porting tsc:
- **Lazy diagnostics** (d6946ff, 40 files): `addLazyDiagnostic` defers its callback outside a file check and `ensurePendingDiagnosticWorkComplete` runs the backlog at the start of `getDiagnostics(file)` and in `getGlobalDiagnostics`; the C# ran them at once. Name-resolution errors of aliases resolved while `initializeTypeChecker` merges the globals then ran their spelling suggestions (`tryResolveAlias` of every global alias) before all scripts were merged and cached the exports of half-merged globals (expando members of global functions missing: 001648, 009549, …; repro: `namespace foo {} import beez = foo.bar; import m2 = no.mod;` / `declare namespace foo {` / `namespace m2 {}` / `function foo() {} foo.x = 1;`).
- **getAlternativeContainingModules** (def4237): not ported before ("Gate C programs have one file"); its search over the enclosing file's imports and then every external module resolves aliases of other files, whose failed resolutions count toward the program-wide `suggestionCount` (the "Did you mean" of 002584 and 4 others). `Program.GetImports` keeps `sourceFile.imports` for it (the file stores stay unwritten). Same commit: the canonical head of spelling-suggestion diagnostics (a later plain 2503 at the same location is a duplicate), `getSpecifierForModuleSymbol` without an enclosing file (the file name of the first non-augmentation declaration: `typeof import("…/000100.ts")`, not the namespace's name).
- e23cbdc: `checkNoTypeArguments` (`T<>` is present: 2315); `checkSourceElement(baseTypeNode.expression)` checks no expression (the C# checked it, so deferred bodies of a merged class's other heritage expression were checked: 17004); `getDeclarationContainer` (`findAncestor(...)!.parent`: variables of scripts, 2661); `getTypeOfDottedName` (the private name `__#<id>@` of `type.symbol`: a merged class symbol finds no member, 2775); `getESSymbolLikeTypeForNode` (`getSymbolOfNode`: one unique symbol for a merged property, 2717); `getIndexInfosOfType` (`getReducedApparentType`: an intersection reduced to never has no index signatures, 2339 with the never elaboration); `combineSymbolTables` (`mergeSymbolTable(combined, first)` takes the merged symbol, the late-bound clone of `__index`; the copy duplicated the index declarations) and `checkTypeForDuplicateIndexSignatures` reading `getMembersOfSymbol` (2374); alias type arguments `[]` versus undefined (`TypeList.PresentEmpty`: a type alias referenced through an import without type arguments has present, empty alias type arguments; inference, relations, `getAliasId`, `isNonGenericTopLevelType` and `getObjectTypeInstantiation` test presence: 74 × 2769 in `external/typescript/src/compiler/visitorPublic.ts`, from `nodeVisitor(node.x, visitor, isTypeNode)` with `visitor: Visitor`).

**Equivalence (`check-equivalence.mjs --corpus tuning-check --skip-tsrs`): identical** — tsc 6.0.3 135,770, C# `cs-o-aot-ws --check --single` 135,770, 0 / 0 differences; typescript-go 7.0.2 single 137,212 (typescript-go's single-threaded count varies on this program between runs: 137,212 / 137,252 / 137,254). No cause outside the checker; no parser, scanner or binder bug, no further unit for the exclusion list.

**Parallel against single** (`--check`, 4 checkers): 130,435 against 135,770 (5,335 fewer; typescript-go 137,140 parallel against 137,212–137,254 single). Per-file comparison of the dumps: (a) 5,342 diagnostics missing in parallel are errors that tsc reports at every declaration of a merged symbol while checking its first declaration — 2393 duplicate function implementation (3,921), 2411 (493), 2814 (389), 2374 (214), 2512 (130), 2394, 2432, 2392, 2312, 2323, 2386, 2413, …: the checker of the first declaration's file adds the errors at declarations in other files, whose diagnostics are read from their own checkers (as in typescript-go: each file's diagnostics come from its checker). Example: a.ts `function f() {}`, b.ts `function f() {}` — single: 2393 in both files; parallel: only a.ts. typescript-go loses few of them because it changed tsc's structure (`checkFunctionOrConstructorSymbol` once per checker on any declaration, not at the first declaration only); the C# keeps tsc's (its single-threaded output is tsc's). Inherent, not a defect. (b) 240 diagnostics at the same position and code with other text: the order in which each checker creates types (union member order, which constituent an elaboration names), the mechanism of the repositories. (c) a few per-checker counters: `suggestionCount` (2552 against 2304), the iteration-type error reported once per union per checker (2488). (d) A defect, fixed (c138f59): each checker added the global diagnostics it produced to its first file's result (tsc's `getDiagnosticsWorker`), so the file-less 2318 for `Disposable`/`AsyncDisposable` appeared four times; the pool now keeps the first copy, as typescript-go's `GetSemanticDiagnostics(ctx, nil)` deduplicates (single-threaded unchanged). No state shared between checkers, no lost per-checker copy found.

**Stability:** `cs-o-aot-ws --check` on tuning-check 4 times: no crash, 130,435 each time, wall 28.0 / 28.3 / 27.4 / 28.1 s (single 30.6 s; other workloads may have run on the machine).

**Gate C:** 3 runs: `--sample 2000` after the first fix (pass), `--sample 2000` after e23cbdc (pass), full corpus at the end (after c138f59): 50,176 of 50,177 identical in all three sections, 0 differing, 027977 timeout under load (accepted). No `src/` change after the full run. **Five repositories** (`check-equivalence.mjs --skip-tsrs`): all identical with tsc 6.0.3 (vscode 101,493, sentry 237,529, bluesky 33,337, playwright 30,808, tldraw 51,900). Files outside `Checker/`: `Program/Program.Resolution.cs` (`GetImports`), `Program/CheckerPool.cs` (the deduplication), this file. C# edited with the file tools only; temporary probes removed before committing.

### CO1: checker scaling and allocations (2026-10-30)

Brief: [modules/CO1.md](modules/CO1.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, alone, directly on main); commits e67e115, 381c155, 7202718 and this entry. Baseline drivers built from bfa872b before the first change (scratch `CO1/base/`).

**Where the parallel time went (tuning-check, baseline, per-checker probe, removed before committing):** checker constructors 2.75 s each in parallel (742 MB each: initializeTypeChecker's duplicate reports), single 1.7 s; checker check times 8.7 / 24.7 / 22.8 / 9.2 s with equal file shares (5,822 files each); 004644.ts (checker 1) and 004645.ts (checker 2) took 15.1 s each against 3.5 / 3.2 s single-threaded, 005153.ts 3.2 s (checker 1). The two files are not slower because of contention: run one checker after the other (same assignment) they still take 10.4 s; they do about three times the work of the single-threaded checker (17,119 against 5,700 union/intersection property computations, 12.6M against 4.4M constituent lookups, 992k against 535k types): which constituent comes first in a union follows the type ids each checker assigned, and the property computation enumerates the first constituent (tsc's algorithm; typescript-go has the same assignment and structure). Thread-time profile (JIT ws, `dotnet-sampled-thread-time`, checker work items): 46 % of the checker threads' time waiting for the GC; inside `computePropertiesOfUnionOrIntersectionType` 58 %; two checker-wide `Dictionary<(Type, string), SymbolRef>` property caches with 6M entries each, `SymTable` (Dictionary + two lists per table) growth, quadratic `List.Find`s in the duplicate reports and in `checkFlagAgreementBetweenOverloads`. GC ws parallel 2,065/617/12, 8.9 s pause (srv 1.9 s).

**Changes** (all inside `Checker/`; diagnostics unchanged after each, tuning-check single identical with tsc 6.0.3 and parallel identical with the baseline's dump):
- e67e115: `SymTable` as entries in insertion order with a scan up to 8 entries and an open-addressing index above; union/intersection `propertyCache`, `propertyCacheWithoutObjectFunctionPropertyAugment` and `resolvedProperties` in the type's `UnionData` record (tsc's fields), the name tables of `computePropertiesOfUnionOrIntersectionType` reused; amalgamated duplicates indexed by file pair and name, `pushIfUnique` through a set, `addDuplicateDeclarationError` stops once an error has five related entries; transient symbols, union records and symbol links in fixed 4,096-record chunks (no copying growth); `isSourceFileDefaultLibrary` once per file and checker; template-literal matching, `removeStringLiteralsMatchedByTemplateLiterals`, `addRelatedInfo`, `addInheritedMembers`, `getTypeListId` without closures or growth garbage.
- 381c155: `group(overloads, file)` by file number (3.1 s of a single-threaded run); one `(name, symbol)` entry array per table; binder symbol names interned per checker; program diagnostics built directly with the checker's reused message builder; `getCrossProductIntersections` with one buffer.
- 7202718: relation cache keys as values (`RelationKey`: type ids and intersection state, the string only for generic type-reference pairs, whose text cannot equal a plain pair's).

**Measurement** (own, interleaved with the baseline, tuning-check, medians; other workloads may have run on the machine):

| Configuration | Baseline | CO1 | Ratio | Allocated | GC (gen0/1/2), pause |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| aot-ws `--check` (5 runs) | 27.69 s | 20.24 s | 0.731 | 21.7 → 11.3 GB | 2,066/616/12, 8.9 s → 1,205/451/11, 7.2 s |
| aot-ws `--check --single` (5) | 30.68 s | 23.61 s | 0.770 | 12.6 → 7.1 GB | 1,225/361/9, 3.7 s → 742/261/8, 2.9 s |
| aot-srv `--check` (5) | 20.54 s | 14.25 s | 0.694 | 21.7 → 11.4 GB | pause 1.9 → 1.4 s |
| aot-srv `--check --single` (5) | 28.72 s | 21.98 s | 0.765 | | pause 1.5 → 1.2 s |

vscode (3 runs each, against the baseline, at 381c155 / 7202718): aot-ws `--check` 25.54 → 22.53 → 21.95 s (allocation 20.2 → 16.7 GB, pause 13.3 → 10.1 s), `--single` 44.52 → 41.87 s, aot-srv `--check` 15.25 → 13.50 s. Tried and reverted (probes, not committed): symbol-table arrays on the pinned object heap (fewer GCs, more pause: 24.5 s against 19.9 s).

**The remaining gap** (tuning-check, final, per-checker probe): ws checkers 6.9 / 18.2 / 16.2 / 7.2 s, srv 3.7 / 12.9 / 11.3 / 4.0 s; 004644/004645 still take 10.7 s each in ws (4.7 s of it GC pause) and 6.6 s in srv against 2.4 / 2.1 s single (the type-order dependent extra work above), 005153.ts 3.0 s (flow analysis, tsc's linear shared-flow search). (1) **ws GC pause** (7.2 s tuning-check, 10.1 s vscode, all four checkers stopped): proportional to the surviving checker data (heap at the end 4.2 GB, about 1 GB per checker), not to the number of collections: with `DOTNET_GCgen0size=256 MB` (JIT, measurement only) vscode goes from 1,757 to 118 collections and the pause from 8.8 to 9.9 s. The survivors are millions of small managed objects per checker (vscode heap snapshot: 3.3M strings, 1.8M `List<NodeRef>` declaration copies of binder symbols, 1.4M mostly empty `List<Signature>`, 1.2M `SymTable`s, 1.2M `ObjectTypeData`); ws marks and copies them single-threaded, srv in parallel. Removing them needs reference-free checker data (types, member lists and declarations in per-checker arenas) — a larger redesign than this step. (2) **Imbalance** on tuning-check (files 004644/004645/005153), inherent to the file assignment both drivers use. (3) **Repeated per-checker work**: vscode srv checkers 10.0–11.8 s each, 43 s together against 32.5 s single (each checker resolves the same library and global types; tsgo does the same).

**Gate C:** 4 runs: `--sample 2000` after each commit (3 × pass), full corpus at the end: pass, 50,177 of 50,177 units dumped without timeout or error, all three sections identical. No `src/` change after the full run. **Equivalence:** `check-equivalence.mjs --corpus tuning-check --skip-tsrs` identical (135,770); `check-equivalence.mjs --skip-tsrs`: all five repositories identical with tsc 6.0.3 (vscode 101,493, sentry 237,529, bluesky 33,337, playwright 30,808, tldraw 51,900); parallel dumps of the five repositories identical with the baseline's. **Stability:** `cs-o-aot-ws --check` on tuning-check 5 times (the measurement runs): no crash, 130,435 each time. **NoAlloc:** Track O 2,412 of 6,142 methods (checker 38 of 2,606, before 28 of 2,587), 55 exceptions. Files outside `Checker/`: this file only (`Program/CheckerPool.cs` carried the measurement probe, removed). C# edited with the file tools only.

**Orchestrator measurement** (2026-10-30, `bench.mjs --corpus tuning-check`, 20 runs each, rig-compliant, baseline drivers of 581431f against 7202718; `results/phase3/CO1/run1/`): `cs-o-aot-ws-check` 27.57 → 20.10 s, median ratio 0.729 [0.723, 0.731]; `cs-o-aot-ws-check-single` 30.54 → 23.49 s, 0.769 [0.767, 0.770]. Kept (every gate passes, not slower by the 95 % interval). Orchestrator re-ran the Gate C compare on the final full dump: 50,177 of 50,177 identical (diagnostics, types, symbols), 0 crashed.

### CO2: reference-free checker data (2026-10-30)

Brief: [modules/CO2.md](modules/CO2.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, alone, directly on main); commits 5f3e6af, d58405c, edd0ed3, 96757fd, c105f77 and this entry. Baseline drivers built from 0441e34 before the first change (scratch `CO2/base/`). Partial: five steps done, the object-type records and member lists not (below). Layout: [phase3-checker-layout.md](phase3-checker-layout.md).

**Baseline survivors** (JIT ws heap snapshot after checking, the four checkers alive; scratch tool reading `GCBulkNode`/`GCBulkEdge`, edges attributed to their owning type): vscode 3.90 GB in 24.0M objects with 62.6M references, tuning-check 4.01 GB in 15.3M objects with 71.9M references. On tuning-check 44.3M of the references were `SymTable` entry names (strings; the union property caches of 004644/004645), 6.5M transient-symbol names, 2.6M instantiation-cache key strings; on vscode 13.8M entry names, 6.5M transient names, 8.4M edges to 2.5M `List<NodeRef>` declaration copies, 2.8M to 2.0M `List<Signature>`, 1.7M `ObjectTypeData`, 0.7M per-file `int[]` link slot arrays (545 MB).

**Steps** (each: Gate C `--sample 2000`, tuning-check single identical with tsc 6.0.3 and parallel identical with the baseline's dump; measured interleaved, aot-ws, 5 runs each, against the previous step):
1. **Names as ids** (5f3e6af): a `NameTable` per checker (one string per distinct escaped name, dense ids); `SymTable` entries are (name id, `SymbolRef`), so entry arrays hold no references, index by id hash; transient symbols store the id; binder symbol names cached per symbol as ids; the property lookups of relations, union properties and symbol creation pass ids. `--check` 0.907, `--single` 0.959 (against 0441e34); srv 0.924 / 0.970.
2. **Symbol link slots** (d58405c): `SymbolSlots`, one program-wide layout per checker (binder symbols of file f at `bases[f] + index`, regions sized by the new read-only `BinderStore.SymbolRecordCount` — `SymbolCount` misses JSDoc typedef symbols bound after the main pass, which a first version tripped over in Gate C; Gate B run once), transient symbols after them, 16K-int pages on first write; node link maps as one (handle, record) array per file. 0.996 / 0.998. Tried and replaced: one program-wide hash map per node link kind (single 1.011, slower).
3. **Id-string caches as value keys** (edd0ed3): `KeyMap<T>` (int-sequence keys in an arena, open addressing) for unionTypes, unionOfUnionTypes, intersectionTypes, the instantiations of object types, conditional roots, aliases and signatures (one map per kind, the owner first in the key), indexedAccessTypes, the active mappers' caches and subtypeReductionCache; the string's parts encoded one to one (lists with their length, a tag per prefix, getAliasId as nothing or -2/symbol/arguments, so `list` and `list + ""` stay equal as in TypeScript). 0.930 / 0.975.
4. **Declarations not copied** (96757fd): `DeclRef` in the transient record (the binder symbol's chain read in place when shared, else a list of the checker's `DeclarationStore` arena); copying the reference shares the array as TypeScript does; readers get a `Decls?` view. 0.966 / 0.987 (a first version boxed views on the union-property path and counted chains eagerly: single 1.013; fixed before the commit).
5. **Type parameter data as records** (c105f77): struct records in chunks. 0.992 / 1.000.

vscode aot-ws `--check` (3 runs each, all steps interleaved with the baseline): 22.89 s → 21.96 / 21.36 / 20.00 / 18.59 / 18.26 s after steps 1–5 (0.798), GC pause 10.58 → 9.53 / 9.10 / 7.69 / 6.55 / 6.47 s.

**Final against the baseline** (tuning-check, interleaved, medians; other workloads may have run on the machine):

| Configuration | Baseline | CO2 | Ratio | Allocated | GC (gen0/1/2), pause |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| aot-ws `--check` (5 runs) | 20.13 s | 16.11 s | 0.801 | 11.34 → 10.68 GB | 1,204/454/11, 7.23 s → 1,099/406/11, 4.08 s |
| aot-ws `--check --single` (5) | 23.64 s | 21.98 s | 0.930 | 7.12 → 6.76 GB | 742/261/8, 2.91 s → 693/231/8, 1.72 s |
| aot-srv `--check` (3) | 14.40 s | 12.90 s | 0.896 | 11.34 → 10.70 GB | pause 1.40 → 0.97 s |
| aot-srv `--check --single` (3) | 22.01 s | 21.38 s | 0.971 | | pause 1.17 → 1.01 s |
| vscode aot-ws `--check` (3) | 22.89 s | 18.26 s | 0.798 | 16.73 → 15.83 GB | 1,721/494/11, 10.58 s → 1,631/467/10, 6.47 s |

**Survivors after checking** (a forced compacting gen2 collection with the checkers alive, measurement probe in the driver, removed): tuning-check parallel 4.1 → 3.5 GB, that collection 2.1 → 1.0 s; vscode 3.9 → 3.45 GB, 2.15 → 1.21 s; tuning-check single 1.91 → 1.66 GB, 0.92 → 0.47 s. Heap snapshots: vscode 24.0M objects / 62.6M references → 16.6M / 27.5M; tuning-check 15.3M / 71.9M → 8.4M / 14.7M. The bytes fall less than the objects: the data is still there, in reference-free arrays the collector does not trace.

**What is left** (vscode snapshot after step 5, objects): `List<Signature>` 2.0M + `Signature[]` 0.56M, `ObjectTypeData` 1.68M, `SymTable` 1.65M + entry arrays 1.29M + index arrays 0.3M (0.68M of them the checker's copies of binder tables), `List<SymbolRef>` 1.4M + `SymbolRef[]` 1.35M (0.74M signature parameters), `List<IndexInfo>` 1.0M (mostly empty), mappers (`ArrayTypeMapper` 0.58M with 1.1M `Type[]`, simple/composite/deferred 0.45M, `Func<Type>` closures), 1.47M strings (literal values, the binder-name intern dictionary, the tuple and template-literal caches still keyed by strings, module resolution). Next: object types as struct records with their resolved member lists (properties, signatures, index infos, declared variants, base types) as ranges in per-checker arenas — the largest remaining part, a change of the member-list API at about 200 call sites (`GetSignaturesOfType`, `GetPropertiesOfType`, `GetIndexInfosOfType`, `ResolveStructuredTypeMembers`), with identity comparisons of lists (`getInstantiatedSignatures`) to keep; then symbol tables in an arena, signature parameters, mappers as records.

### CO3: object types and member lists without references (2026-10-31)

Brief: [modules/CO3.md](modules/CO3.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, alone, directly on main); commits 244d405, 48946ba and this entry. Baseline drivers built from 8ca2c12 before the first change (scratch `CO3/base/`). Partial: goal steps 1 and 2 done, 3 and 4 tried and not kept, 5 not started (below). Layout: [phase3-checker-layout.md](phase3-checker-layout.md).

**Steps** (each: Gate C `--sample 2000`, tuning-check single identical with tsc 6.0.3 and parallel identical with the baseline's dump; measured interleaved with the previous step, aot-ws, 5 runs each):
1. **Object types as struct records, member lists as arena ranges** (244d405): `ObjectTypeRecord` in the type store's chunks; `ObjectTypeData` is a stack handle whose members are `ref`s to the record's fields, so the ~150 call sites read and write it unchanged and copying it never copies the record. Resolved and declared properties, call and construct signatures, index infos and union `resolvedProperties` are `ItemRange<T>`s of per-kind chunked arenas (`ItemArena<T>`), read as `Items<T>` views (the `List` readers; `Concatenate` is core.ts `concatenate`, `SameAs` array identity, as `getInstantiatedSignatures` and `elaborateDidYouMeanToCallOrConstruct` compare); storing a view of the arena keeps its range, as TypeScript shares the array. The resolved members of unions and intersections are a record referenced from `UnionData` (was a dictionary of objects). `IndexInfo` is a value with an identity id (`new` gives a new id, copies keep it: `enumNumberIndexInfo` and `checkIndexConstraintForIndexSignature` compare by identity). `--check` 16.09 → 15.33 s (0.953), `--single` 22.00 → 21.51 s (0.978) against 8ca2c12; the lists alone (first half) 0.969 / 0.982.
2. **Symbol tables in arenas** (48946ba): `SymTable` is a handle (store, id) to a record of the checker's `SymTableStore` (reached through its `NameTable`, so `new SymTable(Names)` stays); entries and index are power-of-two ranges of chunked arenas with a free list per length, a growing table moves. `==` is the Map's identity (`members != emptySymbols`, `symbols === globals`); undefined stays `SymTable?` in the API (extension members as for `Decls?`) and `IsNone` in record fields (`SymTable.OrNew` for `??= new Map()`). `--check` 15.42 → 14.83 s (0.962), `--single` 21.63 → 21.70 s (1.003, inside the run spread [21.54, 21.76] / [21.64, 22.07]; the first versions were 1.005–1.015 single: fixed with one flat record array, no clearing of reused entry ranges and unchecked lookups inside a table's ranges). The binder-table copies stay: the binder's tables are keyed by per-file text references, the checker looks names up by its name ids, and a copy is now arena data, not objects.

**Tried and not kept** (reverted, not committed): signature parameters as arena ranges (`ParametersOf` reading through the arena on the hot paths): `--single` 1.011, `--check` 1.018 against step 2. Array and deferred mappers with their sources and targets as `TypeList`s (removes 1.0M `Type[]` on vscode): `--single` 1.016, `--check` 1.005. Mappers as records were not attempted beyond that: the deferred mappers' closures (inference contexts) must stay collectable, and a store-side list of them would keep every inference context alive.

**Final against the baseline** (8ca2c12 drivers against 48946ba, interleaved, medians; other workloads may have run on the machine):

| Configuration | Baseline | CO3 | Ratio | Allocated | GC (gen0/1/2), pause |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| aot-ws `--check` (5 runs) | 16.16 s | 14.94 s | 0.924 | 10.68 → 9.99 GB | 1,096/397/11, 4.08 s → 745/230/9, 2.43 s |
| aot-ws `--check --single` (5) | 22.02 s | 21.75 s | 0.988 | 6.76 → 6.54 GB | 693/229/8, 1.72 s → 572/164/7, 1.01 s |
| aot-srv `--check` (3) | 13.46 s | 13.52 s | 1.005 | | pause 0.98 → 0.61 s (spread [13.07, 14.29] / [13.16, 13.85]) |
| aot-srv `--check --single` (3) | 21.45 s | 21.30 s | 0.993 | | pause 0.93 → 0.53 s |
| vscode aot-ws `--check` (3) | 18.28 s | 15.38 s | 0.842 | 15.83 → 15.67 GB | 1,632/477/10, 6.57 s → 1,472/430/9, 3.44 s |

**Survivors after checking** (forced compacting gen2 collection with the checkers alive, measurement probe in the driver, removed): tuning-check parallel 3.51 → 3.57 GB, that collection 0.96 → 0.45 s; single 1.66 → 1.68 GB, 0.45 → 0.25 s; vscode 3.45 → 3.42 GB, 1.23 → 0.59 s. Heap snapshots (JIT ws): vscode 16.6M objects / 27.5M references → 5.6M / 12.1M; tuning-check 8.4M / 14.7M → 4.1M / 8.9M. The bytes stay: the data is in reference-free arrays.

**What is left** (vscode snapshot after step 2, objects): strings 1.47M (literal values 0.61M, the name tables' 0.54M, string-keyed literal-type caches 0.56M), type mappers (`ArrayTypeMapper` 0.58M with 1.04M `Type[]`, simple 0.23M, composite 0.14M, deferred 0.08M with `Func<Type>` closures and inference contexts), signature parameter arrays 0.51M, diagnostics (tuning-check: 0.36M `ProgramRelatedInformation`, 0.25M `ProgramDiagnostic`), the instantiable kinds' data classes. The two attempts above show that on tuning-check the remaining kinds cost more CPU through an arena than their collection costs; they pay off where they survive in large numbers (vscode) or with records that avoid the extra indirection on the hot paths (`getMappedType`, `getTypeAtPosition`).

**Gate C:** 4 runs of the 20: `--sample 2000` after each step (2 × pass), the full corpus at the end and the compare re-run on that dump for its summary: `{"mode":"types","sections":["diagnostics","types","symbols"],"units":50177,"identical":50177,"differing":0,"crashed":0,"outcomeMismatch":0,"identicalPerSection":{"diagnostics":50177,"types":50177,"symbols":50177},"library":"identical"}`. No `src/` change after the full run. **Equivalence:** `check-equivalence.mjs --corpus tuning-check --skip-tsrs` identical (135,770); `check-equivalence.mjs --skip-tsrs`: all five repositories identical with tsc 6.0.3 (vscode 101,493, sentry 237,529, bluesky 33,337, playwright 30,808, tldraw 51,900); parallel dumps of the five repositories identical with the baseline driver's. **Stability:** `cs-o-aot-ws --check` on tuning-check 4 times: no crash, 130,435 each time. **NoAlloc:** Track O 2,440 of 6,277 methods (checker 66 of 2,741). ### CO4: parallel check on small programs (2026-10-31)

Brief: [modules/CO4.md](modules/CO4.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, alone, directly on main); commits 5e14304, bb2861c, d9b8bdc and this entry. Baseline drivers built from 4e61cc6 before the first change (scratch `CO4/base/`). Small programs: the first 1,500 and 2,500 files of the tuning-check list (`tc1500`, `tc2500`; 0.9 and 1.2 MB of source, 1,501 and 2,479 program files with the default library), scratch `CO4/lists/`.

**Where the parallel time of a small program goes** (tc2500, aot-ws `--check`, baseline; a timeline probe in the driver, pool and checker — per phase wall, GC pause, per-thread CPU (`GetThreadTimes`) and allocation, per-file check times — env-gated, never committed, removed; typescript-go's phases from a scratch copy of `harness/bench/go/parse.go` with timestamps around `NewProgram`, `BindSourceFiles`, `GetTypeChecker`, `GetSemanticDiagnostics`, `GetGlobalDiagnostics`):

| Phase | Track O (threads) | wall | of it ws GC pause | typescript-go |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| runtime start, before the driver | 1 | ~25 ms | | |
| parse (driver) | thread pool (~15 cores) | 24 ms | 1 ms | program (parse + resolution, parallel) 89 ms |
| program construction | 1 (serial): default library parsed and bound on demand 38 ms, root files and their resolutions 13 ms | 61 ms | 3 ms | |
| bind | thread pool | 16–25 ms | 7 ms | 16 ms |
| checker construction | 4: 20 MB allocated each (globals merge 10.5 MB, amalgamated duplicates 9.3 MB); 19 ms single-threaded | 86 ms | 51 ms | 56 ms |
| check | 4: checkers end at 205 / 311 / 600 / 646 ms; thread CPU 125 / 235 / 516 / 469 ms; GC pause inside each window 68 / 91 / 157 / 164 ms | 646 ms | ~165 ms | 520 ms |
| global diagnostics, deduplication | 4 / 1 | 1 ms | | 0 |
| after the driver's end (process exit) | | ~100 ms | | (outside − inside ≈ 150 ms) |
| **total** | process CPU 2.0 s / 0.84 s = **2.4 cores** (check phase 2.1) | 840 ms inside, 0.96 s outside | 230 ms (27 %) | 690 ms inside, 0.84 s outside |

What keeps cores idle: (1) **the Workstation GC** — 75 gen0 + 28 gen1 collections in 0.85 s (concurrent ws GC caps the gen0 budget at ~6 MB, so the four checkers' 640 MB trigger one every ~8 ms), 2.2 ms each on average, every one stopping all four checkers: 230 ms of the 840, about 480 core-ms in the check phase. The pause follows the surviving data, not the number of collections: with `DOTNET_gcConcurrent=0` (measurement only) 8 + 5 collections and still 205 ms. typescript-go's GC on the same program: 14 concurrent collections on the idle cores (`GODEBUG=gctrace=1`). Server GC: 85 ms pause, 0.84 s wall (ws 0.98 s). (2) **imbalance at the end of the pool**: the sum of the checker walls is 1,762 of 4 × 646 ms (≈ 820 idle core-ms): checker 0 checks `lib.dom.d.ts` (300 ms in the parallel run, 209 ms alone — typescript-go's whole library check of a one-file program: 206 ms, so no gap there) and checker 3 the two expensive units 001245.ts (271 ms) and 000599.ts (146 ms); the assignment i % 4 and the per-checker order fix both. (3) **serial program construction**: 61 ms (7 %) on tc2500; on bluesky (modules) 187 ms, of it 124 ms module resolution of the root files, against typescript-go's 124 ms for parse + resolution in parallel. (4) checker construction: 86 ms against 19 ms single-threaded, two thirds GC pause (each checker merges the globals of all script files and creates the duplicate-declaration errors of all files, three quarters of them in other checkers' files and never read). tc1500: same picture (parse 17, program 53, construction 77 of it 35 GC, check 474 ms; GC 160 of 630 ms). bluesky (account check): parse 40, program 187, bind 10, construction 10, check 770 ms (checker 0: lib.dom 299 + 470 ms; 131 ms GC pause in its window); typescript-go program 124, bind 27, check 593 ms. The "2.2 cores" of the brief: the GC stops (all threads), the tail of the pool (one or two checkers running) and the serial program construction, in this order.

**Steps** (each: Gate C `--sample 2000`; parallel dumps of tc2500, bluesky (and playwright, tuning-check) identical with the baseline's, single dumps identical; measured interleaved, aot-ws, probe builds against the previous step, medians):
1. **Each work item constructs its own checker** (5e14304): typescript-go's createCheckers constructs all checkers and waits for the slowest before any check; the pool now creates checker i on the work item that then checks its files (`CheckerAt`). Neutral: tc1500 1.016 (inside the spread), tc2500 1.007, bluesky 0.989, tuning-check 1.001. Kept as the structure the later steps use (the construction no longer waits for the slowest constructor; the skew was 1–35 ms).
2. **Imports resolved after the program's walk, in parallel** (bb2861c): with noResolve the resolutions add no files, so the walk only lists the files and `Program.ResolveImportedModules(singleThreaded)` resolves them before binding — one work item per file in the pool (typescript-go's file loader resolves each file's imports in its task), serially in `--single` and in a checker's constructor when no pool did (Gate C: before binding, as in the walk). The resolution cache is concurrent (an entry is the same whichever file computes it). bluesky 1.077 → 1.024 s (0.951; resolution 124 ms serial → ~75 ms, of it ~45 ms GC pause promoting the just-parsed files), tc1500 0.989, tc2500 1.002, tuning-check 0.990, tc2500 single 1.001.
3. **A pool checker keeps only its own files' diagnostics** (d9b8bdc): `DiagnosticCollection.KeptFiles` (the files i % 4 == the checker's index) drops the diagnostics a checker produces in other files — the duplicate declarations of merged globals that initializeTypeChecker reports at every declaration, errors at other declarations of a merged symbol — which nothing reads but which lived until the end and were copied by every collection; `addDuplicateDeclarationError` returns before creating an error that would not be kept (a first version without that return re-created the dropped errors with their related information: tuning-check +0.4 GB allocated). Single-threaded and Gate C checkers keep all files. tuning-check 15.09 → 14.16 s (allocated 10.0 → 9.6 GB, GC 746/231 → 697/215, pause 2.41 → 1.91 s), tc1500 0.664 → 0.634 s, tc2500 0.901 → 0.830 s, bluesky 0.989.

After step 3 (tc2500): checker construction 57 ms (was 86; 31 ms of it GC), check 629 ms, GC pause 200 ms (was 230), total inside 785 ms.

**Final against the baseline** (4e61cc6 drivers against d9b8bdc, clean builds, interleaved, medians; other workloads may have run on the machine):

| Configuration | Baseline | CO4 | Ratio | Allocated | GC (gen0/1/2), pause |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| tc1500 aot-ws `--check` (9 runs) | 0.675 s | 0.629 s | 0.932 | 0.56 → 0.54 GB | 54/20/5, 164 ms → 52/19/5, 142 ms |
| tc2500 aot-ws `--check` (9) | 0.895 s | 0.832 s | 0.929 | 0.77 → 0.74 GB | 75/28/6, 231 ms → 71/28/5, 204 ms |
| tc1500 aot-ws `--check --single` (5) | 1.147 s | 1.146 s | 0.999 | | |
| tc2500 aot-ws `--check --single` (5) | 1.539 s | 1.528 s | 0.993 | | |
| tc1500 aot-srv `--check` (5) | 0.599 s | 0.596 s | 0.996 | | pause 70 → 62 ms |
| tc2500 aot-srv `--check` (5) | 0.754 s | 0.725 s | 0.961 | | |
| bluesky aot-ws `--check` (5) | 1.075 s | 0.997 s | 0.927 | 0.82 → 0.85 GB | 164 → 184 ms |
| tuning-check aot-ws `--check` (5) | 14.85 s | 14.26 s | 0.960 | 9.99 → 9.59 GB | 745/237/9, 2.34 s → 697/225/9, 1.93 s |
| tuning-check aot-ws `--check --single` (5) | 21.53 s | 21.83 s | 1.014 | 6.54 GB | 1.01 → 0.98 s |
| tuning-check aot-srv `--check` (3) | 12.89 s | 12.69 s | 0.984 | | pause 0.58 → 0.43 s |
| tuning-check aot-srv `--check --single` (3) | 21.43 s | 21.58 s | 1.007 | | |

**tuning-check `--single` is 1.0–1.4 % slower** in three repetitions (21.53 / 21.56 / 21.51 s against 21.83 / 21.78 / 21.78 s). Per commit (5 runs): step 1 1.003, step 2 1.011, step 3 1.013. Nothing on the single-threaded path changed except where the (same, serial) resolution runs and a null test per diagnostic; the resolution cache back as a plain dictionary (experiment) gave 1.007; the timeline of the probe builds shows the program and construction phases equal and the difference inside the check loop, whose code did not change. Not explained; most likely code placement of the AOT build. tc1500/tc2500 single: 0.999 / 0.993.

**Follow-up on the single-threaded loss** (2026-11-01, asked by the orchestrator after its measurement: `cs-o-aot-ws-check-single` 1.011 [1.009, 1.012], parallel 0.964 [0.957, 0.975]). Clean AOT ws builds of every commit, tuning-check `--check --single`, interleaved, 7 runs each, two rounds (medians; ratio to 4e61cc6): 5e14304 0.995 / 1.004, bb2861c 1.010 / 1.008, d9b8bdc 1.011 / 1.014 — the loss comes with step 2, whose single-threaded path does the same serial resolution at another place (no resolver or cache call while checking; the checker's lookups read the same dictionaries). **Evidence that it is code placement:** (a) a never-executed branch (`if (files.Count < 0) throw …`) added to the checker's constructor moves the time by as much as step 2 does, in both directions — step 1 + the branch 1.010 (step 1 alone 1.004), final + the branch 1.007 (final alone 1.014), same round; (b) the JIT drivers of 4e61cc6 and cbf94c6, same IL, no loss: 25.89 → 25.78 s (0.996, 5 runs each); (c) sampled thread-time profiles of the two JIT drivers (single, one trace each) show the same checker methods with the same sample counts within noise (GetTypeAtFlowNode 1,110 / 1,161, CreateUnionOrIntersectionProperty 447 / 457, InstantiateTypes 331 / 378, CheckFunctionOrConstructorSymbol 242 / 262; the differences are file I/O of the first run); (d) the probe builds' timelines (earlier in this section) show the program and construction phases unchanged and the difference inside the check loop, whose code no step changed. No source change: a layout found by trying placements would not be a fix. No Gate C run used.

**Tried and not kept:** the symbol slot pages (64 KB, small object heap) as 128 KB pages on the large object heap, so that the collector does not copy them: no change of the pause (tc2500 1.017, pause 232 → 230 ms). Not attempted: parsing and binding the default library in parallel during program construction (38 ms serial) — Program.Create does not know whether the run is single-threaded (the driver passes that only to the pool), so a parallel library load would also run in `--single`.

**What is left:** (1) the **Workstation GC pause** is the largest item on small programs (25 % of the wall time of tc2500 after CO4, every collection stopping the four checkers); it follows the data that survives a collection — checker data allocated as small objects (strings, diagnostics, signature parameter arrays, mappers, `Type[]`), the parse and bind data of the program's files promoted during program construction and checker construction, and mid-life objects of the four checkers' work in progress (the gen0 budget of the concurrent ws GC is ~6 MB). Server GC: tc2500 0.73 s, ws 0.83 s. Reducing it needs fewer surviving small objects (CO3's arena attempts for parameters and mappers cost more CPU than they saved on tuning-check) or a runtime configuration decision. (2) **Imbalance**: lib.dom.d.ts (~210 ms of checking, as in typescript-go) and the expensive units sit on fixed checkers; nothing to gain without changing the assignment. (3) The default library parsed and bound serially in program construction (38 ms; parallel only with a single-threaded flag at Program.Create, a driver change). (4) Checker construction repeated per checker (globals merge, 31 ms of GC on tc2500). (5) The CO3 leftovers (strings, mappers, signature parameters, diagnostic objects) unchanged.

**Gate C:** 4 runs of the 12: `--sample 2000` after each commit (3 × pass), the full corpus at the end (pass) and the compare on that dump: `{"mode":"types","sections":["diagnostics","types","symbols"],"units":50177,"identical":50177,"differing":0,"crashed":0,"outcomeMismatch":0,"identicalPerSection":{"diagnostics":50177,"types":50177,"symbols":50177},"library":"identical"}`. No `src/` change after the full run. **Equivalence:** `check-equivalence.mjs --corpus tuning-check --skip-tsrs` identical (135,770); `check-equivalence.mjs --skip-tsrs`: all five repositories identical with tsc 6.0.3 (vscode 101,493, sentry 237,529, bluesky 33,337, playwright 30,808, tldraw 51,900); parallel dumps of the five repositories identical with the baseline driver's (and of tuning-check, tc2500). **Stability:** `cs-o-aot-ws --check` on tuning-check 4 times and `cs-o-aot-srv --check` twice: no crash, 130,435 each time. **NoAlloc:** Track O 2,440 of 6,279 methods (checker 66 of 2,742). Files outside `Checker/`: `Program/CheckerPool.cs`, `Program/Program.cs`, `Program/Program.Resolution.cs`, `Program/ModuleNameResolver.cs`, this file, [phase3-checker-layout.md](phase3-checker-layout.md); the driver carried the timeline probe (env-gated, never committed, removed). C# edited with the file tools only.

**Orchestrator measurement** (2026-10-31, `bench.mjs --corpus tuning-check`, 20 runs each, rig-compliant, baseline drivers of 4e61cc6 against cbf94c6; small programs = the first 1,500 and 2,500 lines of `artifacts/bench/filelists/tuning-check.txt`, hyperfine 20 runs each; `results/phase3/CO4/run1/`): `cs-o-aot-ws-check` 14.72 -> 14.19 s, median ratio 0.964 [0.957, 0.975]; `cs-o-aot-ws-check-single` 21.47 -> 21.70 s, 1.011 [1.009, 1.012] (slower by the interval); tc1500 `--check` 0.663 -> 0.625 s, 0.944 [0.932, 0.954]; tc2500 0.879 -> 0.823 s, 0.936 [0.922, 0.953]. Orchestrator re-ran the Gate C compare on the final full dump: 50,177 of 50,177 identical, 0 crashed. The single-threaded loss violates the keep rule; after the follow-up above (loss arrives with step 2, no extra work found, a never-taken branch moves it by the same amount, the JIT build shows none) **the author decided on 2026-11-01 to keep CO4** as a documented exception: the single-threaded loss of about 1 % is taken as AOT code placement, the parallel gain as removed work.

### CO5: Workstation GC pause on small programs (2026-11-01)

Brief: [modules/CO5.md](modules/CO5.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, alone, directly on main); commit f9d3d81 and this entry. Baseline drivers built from 0042236 before the first change (scratch `CO5/base/`). Small programs: tc1500, tc2500 (the first 1,500 / 2,500 lines of the tuning-check list, as in CO4), bluesky for the account only. Result: step 1 kept, step 2 reverted; the pause of the small programs is almost unchanged (below).

**Account of the pause (goal 1).** Tools: a GC probe in the driver and pool (env-gated, never committed, removed): phase marks with GC counts and OS thread ids, and a polling thread that records every collection of the AOT ws run from `GC.GetGCMemoryInfo` (generation, concurrent, pause, promoted bytes, generation sizes; the AOT drivers have no EventPipe, `dotnet-trace` cannot attach); and JIT ws traces (`dotnet-trace`, GC verbose + `GCHeapSurvivalAndMovement` + `GCSampledObjectAllocationHigh` + type names) read by a scratch tool that follows each sampled object through the survival and move ranges of every collection, so that the promoted bytes of each collection are attributed to type, allocation site (call stack) and origin (the phase and thread that allocated the object). AOT ws, `--check`, medians of 5 runs (tc2500, tc1500) and 3 (bluesky), pause per run:

| Phase | tc2500 | tc1500 | bluesky |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| parse, program construction, resolution, bind | 14 ms (7 %) | 5 ms | 54 ms (28 %; resolution 47 ms: ~60 MB of path strings of the parallel module resolution and the just-parsed files promoted) |
| checker construction | 38 ms (18 %): gen0 14 / gen1 24 ms | 30 ms (20 %) | 10 ms |
| check, four checkers | 81 ms gen0 + 72 ms gen1 = 153 ms (74 %) | 111 ms (75 %) | 127 ms (66 %) |
| background gen2 (BGC suspensions) | 2 ms (1 %) | 4 ms | 5 ms |
| **total** | **208 ms**, 72 collections, 464 MB promoted | 148 ms, 53 | 193 ms, 88 |

Gen2 and background work are about 1 % of the pause; the rest is gen0 and gen1 collections. Per collection (tc2500, check phase, 208 gen0 and 85 gen1 collections over 5 runs): gen0 1.9 ms on average for 0.75 MB promoted, gen1 4.3 ms for 3.0 MB; a fit gives gen0 ≈ 1.0 ms + 1.25 ms/MB, gen1 ≈ 1.6 + 0.9 ms/MB, the fixed part growing with the number of running checkers (0.2 ms with one, 1.7 ms with four). Promoted in the check phase (heap statistics, per run): 31 MB by gen0 and 56 MB by gen1 collections; 97 % of it is reached through the card table from older objects ("Older" mark roots: new checker data stored into long-lived stores), 1–3 % from the four stacks (work in progress); only ~10 MB of the sampled promotions die again in gen1 (mid-life). By type and site (sampled, tc2500, all collections, each promotion counted): two thirds of the promoted bytes are reference-free arrays (`Int32[]` 29.5 MB — symbol slot pages, `KeyMap` arenas, relation stacks; `Type[]` 12.8 — type-list arena growth, union lists, instantiation arguments; `NodeRef[]` 5.6; `SymbolRef[]` 3.8; dictionary entry arrays of the node links and caches), which cost little per byte (CO4: the slot pages on the large object heap did not change the pause); one third (41 MB) holds references: type mappers (`ArrayTypeMapper` 4.3, `SimpleTypeMapper` 1.7, `CompositeTypeMapper` 1.5, the mapper table 0.9 MB; ~190K mapper objects per run, ~95 % of them surviving both promotions) — the largest group in the check phase; the instantiable kinds' data and their `object[]` table (5.4), `ProgramDiagnostic`s (1.6), `ConditionalData`, the expression links (whose record holds a `HashSet`), literal and signature records in doubling arrays; in checker construction the duplicate-declaration info of `reportMergeSymbolError` (~8 MB: per symbol two `HashSet<NodeRef>`, two lists, a dictionary per file pair — built by every checker for every file pair, three quarters for files it does not keep) and the name table. Origins of the promoted bytes (sampled, tc2500, 123 MB in all collections): check 69 %, checker construction 20 %, parse/program/resolution/bind 11 %; bluesky: resolution 11 %. The test of what removes the largest share is step 1: 8–16 % fewer allocations removed 10–20 % of the collections but only 2–5 % of the pause — the pause follows the surviving data with references (mappers first), not the number of collections.

**Steps** (Gate C `--sample 2000` after each; parallel dumps of tc1500, tc2500, bluesky and single dumps of tc1500/tc2500 identical with the baseline's; measured interleaved, aot-ws):
1. **Fewer allocations of work in progress** (f9d3d81): type lists read in place instead of `ToArray` copies in the relater's union/intersection relations, getNormalizedType, isDeeplyNestedType and isGenericTypeWith* (the type-list arena only appends, so a span over an older array stays a valid snapshot); the relater's source/target stacks created only with `maybeKeys`; onSuccessfullyResolvedSymbol and reportErrorsFromWidening run their callback without creating it when lazy diagnostics are eager; markNodeAssignments with one child list and callback per call; getFlowTypeOfReference's state reused from a per-checker stack, getTypeAtFlowNode without a closure; inference and predicate delegates created once; getDeclaredTypeOfClassOrInterface without list growth; comment directives read the file text in place (bluesky: 39 MB of per-file strings). Against the baseline: tc1500 0.995, tc2500 1.001, bluesky 0.970, tuning-check `--check` 0.989, `--single` 0.988.
2. **Fewer surviving objects with references** (tried, reverted): duplicate-declaration locations without per-symbol sets (a short list is searched, the set created at 16 entries), the expression links without a reference (the nonexistent-property cache in a map of the checker), the instantiable data, literal and signature records and the mapper table in `Chunks` instead of doubling arrays, getRecursionIdentity as a value instead of a boxed object, the bind diagnostics read without creating the file's list (a read-only `BinderStore` accessor; Gate B passed). Against step 1: tc2500 0.978 (pause 197 → 188 ms), tc1500 0.996, bluesky 0.999, tuning-check `--check` 0.987, but `--single` 1.007 / 1.010 / 1.003 / 1.006 / 1.010 in five rounds (7 runs each). A no-op control (a never-taken branch in the checker's constructor) did not move either build (step 1 + branch 1.002, step 2 + branch 0.996 of step 2), and every subset of the step was as slow or slower (without the signature chunks 1.015, without all chunk changes 1.011, without the recursion identity 1.016, without the duplicate sets 1.012), so neither a single cause nor code placement could be shown: reverted under the keep rule (patches in scratch `CO5/step2-wip/`).

**Final against the baseline** (0042236 drivers against f9d3d81, interleaved, medians; tc lists 9 runs, others 5, srv tuning-check 3):

| Configuration | Baseline | CO5 | Ratio | Allocated | GC (gen0/1/2), pause |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| tc1500 aot-ws `--check` | 0.640 s | 0.636 s | 0.995 | 0.54 → 0.50 GB | 52/19/5, 144 ms → 46/18/5, 140 ms |
| tc2500 aot-ws `--check` | 0.833 s | 0.834 s | 1.001 | 0.74 → 0.69 GB | 71/25/5, 204 ms → 64/26/5, 197 ms |
| bluesky aot-ws `--check` | 1.003 s | 0.973 s | 0.970 | 0.85 → 0.71 GB | 87/29/6, 184 ms → 70/25/6, 175 ms |
| tc1500 / tc2500 aot-ws `--single` | 1.132 / 1.532 s | 1.142 / 1.525 s | 1.009 / 0.996 | | |
| tc1500 / tc2500 aot-srv `--check` | 0.567 / 0.733 s | 0.566 / 0.734 s | 0.999 / 1.001 | | 62 → 58 / 85 → 90 ms |
| tuning-check aot-ws `--check` | 14.28 s | 14.13 s | 0.989 | 9.59 → 9.05 GB | 699/223/9, 1.95 s → 632/203/9, 1.88 s |
| tuning-check aot-ws `--check --single` | 21.75 s | 21.48 s | 0.988 | 6.54 → 6.03 GB | 1.01 → 0.96 s |
| tuning-check aot-srv `--check` | 12.76 s | 12.51 s | 0.981 | 9.61 → 9.06 GB | 0.44 → 0.41 s |
| tuning-check aot-srv `--check --single` | 21.66 s | 21.27 s | 0.982 | | |

The brief's target for tc1500/tc2500 (clearly faster, less pause) is not reached: −3 % pause, wall unchanged within the spread.

**What is left:** (1) the type mappers — the largest group of surviving objects with references in the check phase (~190K per tc2500 run, nearly all long-lived): mappers as records of a per-checker store (kind, source/target, the two mapper handles; the array kinds' `Type[]` in a side table, the deferred/function mappers' delegates as objects) would remove the objects; it changes `TypeMapper` from a class to a handle at ~200 uses and `getMappedType`'s dispatch (CO3's partial attempt cost CPU). (2) The checker construction's duplicate-declaration info (18 % of the tc2500 pause; step 2's sets helped there) is built by every checker for all file pairs; the outputs land in any file of the declarations, so it cannot be skipped per checker without changing the count rule of `reportAmalgamatedDuplicates`. (3) The rest of the surviving data is spread (instantiable data, diagnostics, caches); reference-free arrays dominate the bytes but not the pause. (4) bluesky's parallel resolution: path strings (`CombinePaths`/`GetDirectoryPath` per ancestor directory, `TsPath` is in `Core/`) and the promotion of the parsed files. (5) Step 2's parallel gain (tc2500 0.978, tuning-check 0.987) is available if its single-threaded 0.3–1 % is accepted.

**Gate C:** 4 runs of the 12: `--sample 2000` after step 1 (pass) and after step 2 (pass, reverted), the full corpus at the end (pass) and the compare on that dump: `{"mode":"types","sections":["diagnostics","types","symbols"],"units":50177,"identical":50177,"differing":0,"crashed":0,"outcomeMismatch":0,"identicalPerSection":{"diagnostics":50177,"types":50177,"symbols":50177},"library":"identical"}`. No `src/` change after the full run. **Gate B** once (step 2): pass. **Equivalence:** `check-equivalence.mjs --corpus tuning-check --skip-tsrs` identical (135,770); `check-equivalence.mjs --skip-tsrs`: all five repositories identical with tsc 6.0.3 (vscode 101,493, sentry 237,529, bluesky 33,337, playwright 30,808, tldraw 51,900); parallel dumps of the five repositories identical with the baseline driver's. **Stability:** `cs-o-aot-ws --check` on tuning-check 4 times and `cs-o-aot-srv --check` twice: no crash, 130,435 each time. **NoAlloc:** Track O 2,440 of 6,284 methods (checker 66 of 2,747). Files outside `Checker/`: `Program/Program.cs`, this file; the driver and `Program/CheckerPool.cs` carried the measurement probe (env-gated, never committed, removed). C# edited with the file tools only.

## Exploration CN1: more than 4 checkers (2026-11-01/02, not pre-registered)

Asked by the author after Phase 3 closed, done by one subagent with nothing committed (raw data and patch in the session scratch, `CN1/`).
- **Switch:** a measurement switch for the checker count N on both sides. typescript-go has its own: `CompilerOptions.Checkers`, with the same assignment, file *i* to checker *i* mod N.
- **Lists:** tuning-check, tc1500/tc2500 and the five repository programs.
- **Values:** N = 4, 8, 12, 16, on aot-ws, aot-srv and typescript-go.
- **Method:** hyperfine, 10 runs per configuration in two rounds (N ascending, then descending).

Geometric mean over the five repositories:

| N | ws vs its N = 4 | srv vs its N = 4 | tsgo vs its N = 4 | ws vs tsgo same N | srv vs tsgo same N |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 4 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 0.997 | 0.836 |
| 8 | 0.841 | 0.745 | 0.708 | 1.183 | 0.879 |
| 12 | 0.825 | 0.669 | 0.628 | 1.310 | 0.890 |
| 16 | 0.888 | 0.681 | 0.603 | 1.468 | 0.944 |

**Workstation GC stops scaling.** Its pause grows with N (vscode 3.4 → 6.5 s from N = 4 to 16), and it never keeps more than about 6 cores busy. Server GC keeps 9–13 cores busy.

**Diagnostic counts change with N on both sides** (as 4 checkers against one, C12) and are deterministic.

**Fixed assignment can backfire.** With *i* mod N, tuning-check at N = 12 was slower than at N = 4: heavy units landed on one checker.

**A crash with 8 or more checkers** was a port defect that 4 checkers can also reach. A spelling suggestion for a global type looked up without a location (`Disposable` from a `using` declaration) became a node diagnostic at no node instead of tsc's global `createError`. Fixed in 6bd5cd6; Gate C full: 50,177 of 50,177 identical.

**Follow-up (author, 2026-11-02): draft amendment `prereg/v10`, §18.** A secondary comparison with 12 checkers on both sides:
- configurations `tsgo7-check12`, `cs-o-aot-ws-check12`, `cs-o-aot-srv-check12`, in profile `check12` with `tsrs-check` and the single-threaded binding rows of profile `check`;
- driver option `--checkers N` in both drivers;
- Table 2f in report.mjs.

The report of additional run 2 is byte-identical with the changed report.mjs. The functional check `bench.mjs --check --profile check12` passes, and the 4-checker configurations report unchanged driver names and counts.

## CO6: surviving checker objects under Workstation GC at 4 and 12 checkers (2026-11-02)

Brief: [modules/CO6.md](modules/CO6.md). One attempt (Opus 5.5, high effort, alone, directly on main); commits a8d816b, 176406c and this entry. Baseline drivers built from c425c65 before the first change (scratch `CO6/base/`). Lists: tc1500, tc2500 (the first 1,500 / 2,500 lines of the tuning-check list, as in CO4 and CO5) and tuning-check; every configuration at 4 checkers and with `--checkers 12`. Baseline counts at 12 checkers (base driver, recorded first): tuning-check 128,748 (130,435 at 4); tc1500 6,487, tc2500 9,778; vscode 101,471 (101,474 at 4), sentry 237,529, bluesky 33,337, playwright 30,798 (30,789 at 4), tldraw 51,900.

**Account** (AOT ws `--check`, GC probe of CO5 in the driver and pool, env-gated, never committed, removed; pause per run, medians of 5 runs; promoted bytes with references from JIT ws traces read with CO5's tool, its list of reference-free types extended by the new records):

| tc2500 | 4 checkers, base | 4, after | 12 checkers, base | 12, after |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| checker construction (gen0 + gen1) | 37.5 ms | 22.5 ms | 100.4 ms | 53.6 ms |
| check (all checkers running + tail) | 148.4 ms | 110.1 ms | 193.3 ms | 148.8 ms |
| parse, program, resolution, bind, background gen2 | 16.8 ms | 17.6 ms | 20.4 ms | 18.2 ms |
| **total** | **202.7 ms** | **150.2 ms** | **314.1 ms** | **220.6 ms** |
| promoted with references (all collections, sampled) | 43.1 MB | 30.2 MB | 51.9 MB | 39.2 MB |

tc1500: 140.4 → 110.8 ms at 4 checkers (construction 26.0 → 20.1, check 105.1 → 81.5), 213.7 → 153.5 ms at 12 (construction 65.7 → 42.8, check 134.9 → 99.7). At 12 checkers the construction is a third of the pause: every checker merges the globals and collects the duplicate-declaration info. Largest promoted groups with references before (tc2500, 12 checkers): the instantiable kinds' `object[]` table 4.4 MB, `ArrayTypeMapper` 4.0, `SimpleTypeMapper` 1.6, `CompositeTypeMapper` 1.4, the mapper table 1.1, the duplicate-declaration info (pair dictionaries 1.7, `HashSet<NodeRef>` 1.7, symbol dictionaries 1.1, lists, info objects), the name tables' `string[]` 2.8, `SignatureRecord[]` 2.5, the type predicate cache 1.4, the expression links 1.3. After: `object[]` 5.1 (the instantiable kinds' table and the mapper store's object table), the inference contexts the mapper store keeps alive (`InferenceInfo` 1.7, `InferenceContext` 1.1, their lists and arrays ≈ 4 MB), `string[]` 3.0, `SignatureRecord[]` 2.7, `List<Type>` 1.7, the type predicate cache 1.5, the expression links 1.4, `ConditionalData` 1.0, `LiteralData[]` 0.9; no mapper objects and no duplicate-info objects.

**Steps** (Gate C `--sample 2000` after each; tc1500/tc2500 dumps at 4 and 12 checkers and single identical with the baseline's; measured interleaved, aot-ws, tc lists 9 runs, tuning-check 5, against the previous step):
1. **Type mappers as records** (a8d816b): `TypeMapper` is an int handle to a `MapperRecord` (kind and three ints) of the checker's `MapperStore` in 4,096-record chunks; simple: source and target; array kinds: sources and targets copied into 16K-type chunks; composite and merged: the two mapper ids; function and the remaining deferred kind: an index into the store's object table. The fixing and non-fixing mappers of an inference context are kinds of their own that compute their targets from the context (one table slot per context; no closures, no `Func` arrays). Every make* call creates a record, so `==` is the identity of mappers (the active mappers' caches compare ids); the mapper table of signature records and symbol links is gone. `getMappedType` switches on the kind. tc1500 0.940 (pause 141 → 114 ms), `--checkers 12` 0.965; tc2500 0.949 (198 → 164 ms), `--checkers 12` 0.936 (309 → 262 ms); tuning-check `--check` 0.984 (1.89 → 1.55 s), `--checkers 12` 0.960 (3.70 → 3.15 s), `--single` 1.010 / 1.013 / 1.011 / 1.005 in four rounds. A never-taken branch in the checker's constructor (CO4's control) moved `--single` by +0.5 % (baseline) and +1.0 % (step 1) in the same round, and the JIT drivers show no loss (`--single` 0.990, 5 runs): taken as AOT code placement, step kept. Cost: the store's object table keeps every inference context alive (TypeScript keeps one alive while a mapper of it is referenced; before, about a quarter survived).
2. **Duplicate-declaration info without objects** (176406c): `amalgamatedDuplicates` as one `AmalgamatedDuplicates` per checker construction: pair records in insertion order with an index by file numbers, symbol records chained per pair with an index by (pair, name id), location records chained per list with one set of (list, location) for pushIfUnique; same orders, count rule and reports. Sharing it between the checkers was not done: the info is a by-product of each checker's globals merge (its targets are the checker's merged symbols, and `symbolToString` runs in that checker), so building it before the checkers start would need a merge outside the checkers. tc1500 `--checkers 12` 0.957 (179 → 151 ms), tc2500 `--checkers 12` 0.942 (264 → 220 ms); tuning-check `--checkers 12` 0.950 (3.11 → 2.46 s), `--check` 1.000, `--single` 0.993; tc lists at 4 checkers 1.003 / 0.996.

**Tried and reverted:** the expression links without their `HashSet<string>` (the nonexistent-property cache as one set of (node, type, isUncheckedJS) per checker) and the outer type parameters of anonymous types as type-list ranges instead of `Type[]`: tc1500/tc2500 `--checkers 12` 0.979 / 0.985, `--check` 0.997 / 1.008, tuning-check 1.002 / 0.998 / `--single` 1.004; no pause change, so not kept (patch in scratch `CO6/step3-reverted.patch`).

**Final against the baseline** (c425c65 drivers against 176406c, interleaved, medians; tc lists ws 9 runs, single and srv 5, tuning-check ws 5, srv 3 and 7; other workloads may have run on the machine):

| Configuration | Baseline | CO6 | Ratio | Allocated | GC (gen0/1/2), pause |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| tc1500 aot-ws `--check` | 0.673 s | 0.631 s | 0.938 | 0.50 → 0.49 GB | 46/18/5, 146 ms → 45/18/6, 109 ms |
| tc1500 aot-ws `--checkers 12` | 0.610 s | 0.541 s | 0.887 | 0.59 → 0.57 GB | 56/23/6, 216 ms → 54/23/6, 153 ms |
| tc2500 aot-ws `--check` | 0.823 s | 0.767 s | 0.933 | 0.69 → 0.67 GB | 64/25/5, 198 ms → 63/23/5, 146 ms |
| tc2500 aot-ws `--checkers 12` | 0.750 s | 0.667 s | 0.889 | 0.81 → 0.79 GB | 78/31/6, 309 ms → 74/31/6, 217 ms |
| tc1500 / tc2500 aot-ws `--single` | 1.151 / 1.505 s | 1.127 / 1.488 s | 0.980 / 0.989 | | 101 → 72 / 134 → 96 ms |
| tc1500 / tc2500 aot-srv `--check` | 0.573 / 0.730 s | 0.571 / 0.716 s | 0.997 / 0.981 | | 57 → 52 / 90 → 72 ms |
| tc1500 / tc2500 aot-srv `--checkers 12` | 0.468 / 0.558 s | 0.462 / 0.550 s | 0.989 / 0.985 | | 66 → 68 / 96 → 85 ms |
| tuning-check aot-ws `--check` | 14.11 s | 13.62 s | 0.966 | 9.06 → 9.01 GB | 632/211/9, 1.91 s → 613/192/9, 1.40 s |
| tuning-check aot-ws `--checkers 12` | 16.18 s | 14.91 s | 0.922 | 12.26 → 12.22 GB | 800/255/14, 3.78 s → 745/217/9, 2.39 s |
| tuning-check aot-ws `--check --single` | 21.50 s | 21.39 s | 0.995 | 6.03 → 5.99 GB | 511/152/7, 0.98 s → 505/136/7, 0.83 s |
| tuning-check aot-srv `--check` (3 / 7 runs) | 12.50 / 12.49 s | 12.86 / 12.58 s | 1.029 / 1.007 | | 0.40 → 0.35 / 0.42 → 0.31 s (gen2 24 → 27) |
| tuning-check aot-srv `--checkers 12` (3) | 13.11 s | 13.14 s | 1.002 | 12.27 → 12.22 GB | 0.58 → 0.40 s |

The brief's targets: tc1500/tc2500 `--checkers 12` clearly faster with less pause (0.887 / 0.889, pause −29 / −30 %); tuning-check `--check`, `--checkers 12` and `--single` not slower (0.966 / 0.922 / 0.995). `cs-o-aot-srv --check` on tuning-check is **0.7 % slower** in the 7-run repetition (spreads [12.46, 12.62] / [12.52, 12.69]; 1.029 in the 3-run round): Server GC runs more gen2 collections (24 → 27), most likely for the inference contexts the mapper store keeps.

**What is left:** (1) the inference contexts kept by the mapper store's object table (≈ 4 MB of the 39 MB promoted with references on tc2500 at 12 checkers): a context's mappers are ids, so the store cannot tell when a context is unreachable; keeping only the contexts whose mappers reach stored records (types, signatures, links, other contexts) would need a mark at every such store. (2) Checker construction at 12 checkers (54 ms of 221 on tc2500): the slot pages and name tables of the globals merge, repeated by every checker. (3) The rest is spread: the instantiable kinds' data and table, `SignatureRecord[]` (doubling, holds the parameter arrays), the type predicate and JSDoc tag caches (the latter in `Parser/`), literal records, diagnostics. (4) The duplicate-declaration info is still built by every checker; shared, read-only data would need the globals merge before the checkers start.

**Gate C:** 4 runs of the 12: `--sample 2000` after step 1 and after step 2 (pass both), the full corpus at the end (pass) and the compare on that dump: `{"mode":"types","sections":["diagnostics","types","symbols"],"units":50177,"identical":50177,"differing":0,"crashed":0,"outcomeMismatch":0,"identicalPerSection":{"diagnostics":50177,"types":50177,"symbols":50177},"library":"identical"}`. No `src/` change after the full run. **Equivalence:** `check-equivalence.mjs --corpus tuning-check --skip-tsrs` identical (135,770); `check-equivalence.mjs --skip-tsrs`: all five repositories identical with tsc 6.0.3 (vscode 101,493, sentry 237,529, bluesky 33,337, playwright 30,808, tldraw 51,900); parallel dumps of the five repositories and of tuning-check at 4 and at 12 checkers identical with the baseline driver's. **Stability:** `cs-o-aot-ws --check` on tuning-check 4 times (130,435 each time), `--checkers 12` twice (128,748 each time, the baseline count), `cs-o-aot-srv --check` twice (130,435): no crash. **NoAlloc:** Track O 2,445 of 6,296 methods (checker 71 of 2,759). Files outside `Checker/`: this file, [phase3-checker-layout.md](phase3-checker-layout.md); the driver and `Program/CheckerPool.cs` carried the measurement probe (env-gated, never committed, removed). C# edited with the file tools only.

**Orchestrator measurement** (2026-11-02, `bench.mjs --corpus tuning-check`, 20 runs each, rig-compliant, baseline drivers of c425c65 against 381b08e, final first then baseline; tc1500/tc2500 hyperfine 20 runs each, final and base alternating per hyperfine invocation; `results/phase3/CO6/run1/`; median ratio with 95 % bootstrap interval):

| Configuration | Baseline | CO6 | Ratio [95 %] | GC (gen0/1/2), pause |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| tuning-check `cs-o-aot-ws-check` | 13.967 s | 13.652 s | 0.977 [0.973, 0.985] | 632/210/9, 1.91 s → 620/190/9, 1.59 s |
| tuning-check `cs-o-aot-ws-check12` | 15.963 s | 14.585 s | 0.914 [0.908, 0.918] | 799/266/13, 3.71 s → 748/213/10, 2.54 s |
| tuning-check `cs-o-aot-ws-check-single` | 21.412 s | 21.390 s | 0.999 [0.998, 1.001] | 511/151/7, 1.02 s → 505/137/7, 0.81 s |
| tuning-check `cs-o-aot-srv-check` (secondary) | 12.504 s | 12.554 s | 1.004 [1.002, 1.008] | 58/35/28, 0.39 s → 55/36/28, 0.31 s |
| tc1500 `--check` | 0.620 s | 0.594 s | 0.958 [0.948, 0.967] | |
